*Anik Basu Bhaumik,Suman Dutta,Siyi Wang,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将最优Toffoli深度的多控制Toffoli（MCT）门分解映射到基于双变量自行车（BB）码的容错量子架构上，通过外部魔法态工厂直接注入|CCZ⟩态，提出利用MCT分解二叉树结构的定向放置策略，相比朴素顺序首次适应放置减少最高16.02%的模块间指令数，并验证了网格布局相对线性架构可减少最高23.7%的模块间指令，最终通过bicycle_numerics估算器验证了编译电路的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多控制Toffoli门是量子电路设计中的基本原语，但其在BB码架构上的映射存在复杂的编译瓶颈（模块间路由、工厂密度与布局）。BB码虽大幅降低容错开销，但非Clifford电路在模块化BB码架构上的实现尚未被充分探索，MCT门到BB码架构的映射研究仍属空白。

Method: 将Dutta等人提出的最优Toffoli深度MCT分解映射到BB码容错架构，采用外部魔法态工厂直接注入|CCZ⟩态；提出一种利用MCT分解二叉树结构的目标化放置策略，将相互作用的子树共置；同时评估不同拓扑（网格vs线性）下工厂放置对模块间指令数的影响；使用Qiskit社区的bicycle_numerics估算器分析聚合执行错误与逻辑故障概率。

Result: 所提出的二叉树导向放置策略相比朴素顺序首次适应放置减少高达16.02%的模块间指令数；网格布局相对线性架构（Yoder等）减少高达23.7%的模块间指令数；通过bicycle_numerics验证了编译电路的执行错误与逻辑故障概率在可行范围内。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统性地将MCT门最优分解映射到BB码容错架构，证明了利用分解结构特征（二叉树）进行智能放置可显著降低模块间通信开销，且网格拓扑优于线性架构；研究为模块化BB码量子架构上的非Clifford电路编译提供了可行的路径与验证工具。

Abstract: The multi-controlled Toffoli (MCT) gate is a fundamental primitive in quantum circuit design, with applications in quantum arithmetic, cryptanalysis, and algorithmic implementations. Being a high-level logical operation, the efficient decomposition of MCT gates into lower-level netlists has remained a major optimization challenge for decades. While emerging quantum error-correcting codes such as bivariate bicycle (BB) codes drastically reduce fault-tolerance overhead, realizing non-Clifford circuits on modular BB-code architectures introduces complex compilation bottlenecks governed by inter-module routing, factory density, and layout. Consequently, the mapping of MCT gates onto BB-code architectures remains relatively unexplored.
  In this paper, we overcome these challenges by mapping optimal-Toffoli-depth MCT decompositions (Dutta et al., PRA, 2025) onto BB-code-based fault-tolerant architectures via direct $\lvert \mathrm{CCZ} \rangle$ state injection from an external magic state factory. We introduce a targeted placement strategy that exploits the binary-tree structure of MCT decompositions to co-locate interacting subtrees. This approach reduces inter-module instruction counts by up to $\mathbf{16.02}\%$ compared to a naive sequential first-fit placement. We also evaluate the impact of factory placement across different topologies, demonstrating that grid-based layouts yield up to a $\mathbf{23.7}\%$ reduction in inter-module instructions relative to linear architectures (Yoder et al., arXiv, 2025). Finally, we validate the practical viability of our compiled circuits by analyzing aggregate execution errors and logical failure probabilities using the bicycle-ISA error estimator bicycle_numerics provided by the Qiskit community, https://github.com/qiskit-community/bicycle-architecture-compiler.

</details>


### [101] [Towards Natural Gas Contract Selection via Quantum-Guided Independent Set Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00881)
*Vivek Dixit,Vaibhaw Kumar,Kentaro Ohno,Alberto Maldonado Romo,Larry Bowden*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究天然气运输合同选择的优化问题，将成对合同兼容性抽象为最大独立集（MIS）问题，并提出一种结合经典图约简与量子引导优化的量子-经典混合框架，在QOBLIB基准和合成合同兼容图上分别达到0.996和0.989的平均近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然气运输合同选择是实际且重要的优化任务，需在候选合同中选出相互兼容的合同集合，并满足时间、基础设施和流量相关约束。当候选合同数量增长时，搜索空间爆炸，传统穷举方法难以处理。在噪声量子硬件限制下，迫切需要一种能在当前量子设备上运行的大规模MIS求解框架。

Method: 将合同兼容性问题建模为合同兼容图上的最大团问题，等价于补图上的最大独立集（MIS）问题。采用量子-经典混合框架：经典迭代图约简逐步缩减搜索空间，量子引导优化在约简后的子问题上求解MIS，两者循环结合以保持高质量解的同时适应量子硬件的规模限制。

Result: 在QOBLIB的15个基准实例上平均近似比为0.996，恢复14个实例的最优解（图规模最大186个顶点）；在6个合成合同兼容图（最多900个合同）上平均近似比0.989，4个案例获得最优解。验证了混合MIS求解器对工业动机图的缩减能力。

Conclusion: 该量子-经典混合MIS求解器能有效处理大规模工业动机图，作为两阶段筛选流程的第一步，将候选合同缩减到较小的相互兼容集合，后续可结合管道容量约束进行验证，展示了量子计算在实际工业优化问题中的实用价值。

Abstract: Selecting mutually compatible natural gas transportation contracts is a practically important optimization task in which operators must choose from many candidate agreements subject to temporal, infrastructural, and flow-related constraints. As the number of candidates grows, the resulting search space becomes difficult to explore exhaustively. We study a pairwise abstraction of this task, formulated as a Maximum Clique problem on a contract-compatibility graph, or equivalently as a Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem on the complement graph.
  Building on recent work, this paper studies a quantum-classical framework for solving large-scale MIS instances within the limitations of noisy quantum hardware. The approach combines iterative classical graph reduction with quantum-guided optimization to progressively simplify the search space while maintaining high solution quality. This enables large candidate spaces to be reduced to smaller subproblems that are more suitable for execution on current quantum computers.
  We evaluate the approach on fifteen benchmark instances from the Quantum Optimization Benchmarking Library (QOBLIB), obtaining an average approximation ratio of 0.996 and recovering optimal solutions for fourteen instances, including graphs with up to 186 vertices. We further evaluate the algorithm on six synthetic pairwise contract-compatibility graphs containing up to 900 contracts, where the proposed method achieves an average approximation ratio of 0.989 and obtains optimal solutions in four cases. These experiments demonstrate the ability of the hybrid MIS solver to reduce industrially motivated graphs. The pairwise abstraction serves as the first step of a two-stage screening procedure that narrows the candidate contracts to a smaller set of mutually compatible ones, which can then be verified against pipeline-capacity constraints.

</details>


### [102] [Robust CZ gate against flux line memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00939)
*Yao Song,Xiu-Hao Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对超导量子处理器中CZ门因磁通线记忆效应导致传统预失真失效的问题，将磁通线失真建模为有状态经典执行器的动力学，通过一阶Dyson展开推导错误生成器，优化磁通脉冲以抑制这些误差生成器并最小化门出口处的残余磁通线状态，从而在重复CZ门序列中保持高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中高保真CZ门至关重要，但基于磁通调谐的实现对脉冲失真敏感。传统预失真方法对孤立门有效，但当磁通线存在记忆效应时，重复CZ门的保真度会迅速下降，这构成了一个关键限制。

Method: 将磁通线失真建模为有状态经典执行器与量子系统耦合的动力学；利用一阶Dyson展开推导由初始磁通线状态变化引起的误差生成器；通过优化磁通脉冲来抑制这些误差生成器并最小化门出口处的残余磁通线状态。

Result: 单极点磁通线模型显示预期的一阶鲁棒性平台；三极点模型下优化CZ脉冲达到F_avg=99.998%，抑制所有一阶误差生成器并使残余磁通线状态接近零；无额外等待时，十门序列的鲁棒脉冲达到F_avg=99.97%，序列不保真度相比基线降低约2.3×10^3倍。

Conclusion: 显式考虑磁通线记忆效应可以在重复门序列中维持高保真CZ操作，解决了传统预失真的关键局限性。

Abstract: High-fidelity CZ gates are central to superconducting quantum processors, but implementations based on flux tuning are still sensitive to pulse distortion. Conventional predistortion, typically used for an isolated gate, can recover the desired flux at the chip but it fails if the flux line memory exists, causing the fidelity of repeated CZ gates to drop rapidly. To address this issue, we model the flux line distortion as the dynamics of a stateful classical actuator coupled to a quantum system. Using a first-order Dyson expansion, we derive the error generators induced by variations in the initial flux-line state. We then design a robust CZ gate by optimizing the flux pulse to suppress these generators and minimize the residual flux line state at the gate exit. A one-pole flux line model shows the expected first-order robustness plateau. For a more practical three-pole model, the optimized CZ pulse achieves $F_{\mathrm{avg}}=99.998\%$, suppresses all first-order error generators, and brings the residual flux line state close to zero. With no additional waiting time between gates, our robust pulse achieves $F_{\mathrm{avg}}=99.97\%$ for the complete ten-gate sequence and reduces the sequence infidelity by a factor of about $2.3\times10^{3}$ relative to the baseline under the same predistortion protocol. These results show that explicitly accounting for flux line memory maintains high-fidelity CZ operation across repeated gate sequences and addresses a key limitation of conventional predistortion.

</details>


### [103] [Irreducibility and regularisation properties of Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01547)
*Franco Fagnola,Federico Girotti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了作用于连续变量量子系统上的高斯量子马尔可夫半群(GQMSs)的正则化与不可约性性质，建立了正则化效应与漂移/量子扩散矩阵的代数条件之间的联系，并通过多个等价代数准则（包括Hörmander条件的量子类比）刻画了不可约性，发现不可约性严格强于正则化条件——这与经典情形形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要为非经典情形的量子马尔可夫半群建立严格的理论框架，理解高斯量子马尔可夫半群的正则化与结构性质（如不可约性和无退相干子系统），为更广泛的可约GQMS及连续变量系统上的量子马尔可夫半群研究奠基。

Method: 首先提出在该设定下算子的自然正则性概念，进而利用漂移矩阵和量子扩散矩阵的代数条件来刻画GQMS的平滑化效应，并将这些条件与量子线性系统的可控性理论及无退相干子系统结构联系起来。随后通过多个等价的代数准则刻画不可约性，包括漂移/扩散矩阵表述、一般化GKLS表示的算子表述以及量子Hörmander条件式的表述。

Result: 建立了正则在性与不可约性的完整代数刻画框架。核心发现是：与经典情形（扩散情形中不可约性通常可由正则化条件推导）不同，在量子情形中不可约性是比正则化条件严格更强的性质。

Conclusion: 所建立的代数框架为分析可约高斯量子马尔可夫半群及更一般的连续变量系统上的相关量子马尔可夫半群奠定了基础，揭示了量子与经典马尔可夫过程在结构性质上的本质差异。

Abstract: We study regularisation and irreducibility properties of Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups (GQMSs) acting on continuous-variable quantum systems. We first identify a natural notion of regularity for operators in this setting, which allows us to formulate and characterise the smoothing effects of GQMSs in terms of algebraic conditions involving the drift and quantum diffusion matrices. These conditions establish a connection with the controllability theory of quantum linear systems and with the structure of decoherence-free subsystems.
  We then characterise irreducibility through several equivalent algebraic criteria: one formulated in terms of the drift and quantum diffusion matrices, one in terms of the operators appearing in the generalised GKLS representation of the generator, and a third given by a quantum analogue of Hörmander's condition. A central and somewhat surprising consequence is that, in contrast with the classical case, irreducibility is strictly stronger than conditions ensuring regularisation. Our results provide an algebraic framework for analysing these properties and lay the groundwork for a broader study of reducible Gaussian quantum Markov semigroups and, more generally, more general relevant quantum Markov semigroups on continuous-variable systems.

</details>


### [104] [Uniform Hiding of Haar Block Transpose Gram Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01008)
*Hongru Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对具有等强度压缩主动输入的高斯玻色采样，证明了其无碰撞概率与相应复高斯转置Gram矩阵分布之间在有限全变差距离下的比较定理。误差界是显式的，随所选输出模数量呈二次增长，与干涉仪尺寸成反比，且对压缩主动输入数量一致均匀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯玻色采样（GBS）是量子计算优越性的关键候选方案，其计算复杂性论证通常依赖随机矩阵理论中关于概率分布的可替换性假设。然而，对于具有完全对称压缩输入的实际GBS设置，缺乏严格的有界变差范数误差估计来支撑硬度论证。

Method: 证明分为两个部分：（1）在稠密区域采用中心化圆型正交系综（circular orthogonal ensemble）的得分分析，处理干涉仪块对应的随机结构；（2）结合矩形相对熵界，处理稀疏/边缘区域的情况。两部分的结合使得误差界在输出模数上为二次、在干涉仪大小上为反比，且对主动压缩输入数一致。

Result: 得到了有限全变差（total variation）比较结果，即将GBS的无碰撞概率分布逼近为复高斯转置Gram矩阵的对应分布。误差上界是显式的且量化清晰。

Conclusion: 该结果为高斯玻色采样硬度论证提供了随机矩阵替换组件，验证了在特定参数范围内GBS输出分布可以被复高斯随机矩阵分布良好近似，从而支撑基于该替换的复杂性下界结论。

Abstract: Gaussian boson sampling with equally squeezed active inputs assigns collision free probabilities through a complex symmetric transpose Gram matrix formed from a rectangular block of a Haar interferometer. We prove a finite total variation comparison with the corresponding complex Gaussian transpose Gram law. The error bound is explicit, quadratic in the number of selected output modes, inversely proportional to the interferometer size, and uniform in the number of squeezed active inputs. The proof combines a centered circular orthogonal ensemble score analysis in the dense regime with a rectangular relative entropy bound. This result supplies a random matrix replacement component of Gaussian boson sampling hardness arguments.

</details>


### [105] [Nonlocal wavefront shaping through complex media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01017)
*Yanis Trouyet,Neelan Gounden,Pedro Ornelas,Patrick Cameron,Andrew Forbes,Hugo Defienne*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了一种利用空间纠缠光子对的非局域波前整形技术。通过对从未与散射介质相互作用的纠缠光子施加相位校正，即可补偿其纠缠伙伴所经历的散射畸变，恢复两者初始的空间关联，从而将波前校正与散射介质在物理上解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波前整形技术具有内在的局域性——校正元件（如空间光调制器或变形镜）必须直接放置在散射光的传播光路中，这严重限制了其在紧凑成像系统中的集成，尤其在难以触及或空间受限的应用场景中无法发挥作用。

Method: 利用空间纠缠光子对实现非局域波前整形：对未与散射介质相互作用的光子施加相位校正，从而补偿其在散射介质中经历畸变的纠缠伙伴的波前失真，重建两光子间的初始空间关联。

Result: 实验成功证明通过对"旁观者"光子的非局部相位修正即可恢复散射介质的畸变效应，实现了波前整形与散射介质的物理解耦。

Conclusion: 该非局域波前整形方案突破了传统技术对校正元件位置的光学路径限制，为在紧凑和天然不可进入的系统中实现透过复杂介质的成像开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Wavefront shaping is a key technique for mitigating scattering in complex media, enabling advanced imaging and optical communication. Yet existing approaches are inherently local, requiring active correction elements - such as spatial light modulators or deformable mirrors - to lie directly in the optical path of the scattered light, which limits their integration into compact imaging systems. Here, we experimentally demonstrate nonlocal wavefront shaping using spatially entangled photon pairs. By applying a phase correction to a photon that never interacts with the scattering medium, we compensate for the distortions experienced by its entangled partner and restore their initial spatial correlations. Our approach physically decouples the wavefront correction from the scattering medium, paving the way for imaging through complex media in compact and otherwise inaccessible systems.

</details>


### [106] [Photonic fusion operations transform partial distinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01019)
*S. N. van den Hoven,G. B. Lamers,J. J. Renema*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别出光子融合操作的一个此前未被考虑的区别维度：不同的融合实现方式如何变换部分可区分性（partial distinguishability）。研究发现，不同成功的融合事件会因协议、宣告结果和错误模型的不同，对可区分性产生截然不同的变换效果。在集体资源态错误模型下，作者提出对现有融合协议的修改，能平均提高剩余态的保真度；但总体而言，这些变换往往劣化可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子融合操作是光子量子信息处理的核心原语，传统上仅以成功概率和资源成本作为评估标准。本文指出这忽略了不同融合实现在处理部分可区分性上的差异——这种差异源自不完美的纠缠资源态和不完美的单光子，可能对量子信息处理产生重要影响，因此有必要系统地研究融合操作对部分可区分性的动态变换。

Method: 作者系统考察了来自两种误差源的可区分性：（1）不完美的纠缠资源态，（2）不完美的单光子。针对不同的融合协议、宣告结果和错误模型，分析了成功融合事件对可区分性的变换方式。特别地，在集体资源态错误模型下提出对现有融合协议的修改方案以改善剩余态保真度。

Result: 研究发现：不同成功融合事件可引发显著不同的可区分性变换，具体取决于融合协议、宣告结果和错误模型；在集体资源态错误模型下，修改后的融合协议可平均提升剩余态保真度；但总体上这些变换大多对可区分性产生不利影响，许多成功融合结果伴随可区分性劣化。

Conclusion: 部分可区分性会被干涉和测量过程动态塑造，因此在评估光子融合协议时，必须将其与总体融合成功概率一同纳入考量，不能仅以成功概率和资源成本作为评价标准。

Abstract: Photonic fusion operations are a central primitive in photonic quantum information processing and are commonly characterized by their success probability and resource cost. Here, we identify an additional way in which different implementations of fusion operations differ, namely in how they transform partial distinguishability. We consider distinguishability originating both from imperfect entangled resource states and from imperfect single photons. We find that different successful fusion events can induce markedly different distinguishability transformations depending on the fusion protocol, heralding outcome, and error model. Under a collective resource-state error model, we identify a modification to an existing fusion protocol that improves the fidelity of the remaining state on average. Generally, however, these transformations adversely affect distinguishability, and many successful fusion outcomes are accompanied by distinguishability degradation. Our results show that partial distinguishability is dynamically shaped by interference and measurements and should be considered alongside overall fusion success probability when evaluating photonic fusion protocols.

</details>


### [107] [Scattering and Tunneling in Real Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01066)
*Firdevs Karakus,Daniil Stepanenko,Igor Volovich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在实量子力学框架下建立了普适的演化方程，证明了实Kähler空间的Stone定理类比，研究了散射与隧穿问题，并证明其结果与标准复量子力学完全等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实量子力学是一种以实矢量空间为态空间的量子力学形式，其演化算子需同时保持正交与辛结构。但目前缺乏对其一般演化方程、散射理论和隧穿问题的系统研究，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者在实Kähler空间中证明了Stone定理的类比，从而推导出一般演化方程，该方程的演化算子同时是正交和辛的；随后建构了基于实波算子和S矩阵的散射理论，并分析了微分散射截面；同时对隧穿概率进行了推导与比较。

Result: 对于所考虑的一类势函数，实量子力学中的微分散射截面与标准复量子力学结果等价；隧穿概率在两种框架下也完全相同。

Conclusion: 实量子力学在散射与隧穿等核心物理可观测量上与复量子力学给出一致的结果，表明实量子力学是一种自洽且物理上等效的量子力学表述。

Abstract: We establish the general form of the evolution equation and study scattering and tunneling in real quantum mechanics. We prove an analogue of Stone's theorem for a real Kahler space and derive the corresponding general evolution equation, whose evolution operator is both orthogonal and symplectic. We formulate scattering theory in terms of real wave operators and the associated S-matrix. For the class of potentials considered, we show that the differential scattering cross section is equivalent to that obtained in standard complex quantum mechanics. We also show that the tunneling probability in real quantum mechanics is identical to that in complex quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [108] [A continuous-mode quantum-optical representation of finite strong-field laser pulses in free space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01157)
*Szabolcs Hack,Attila Czirják,Péter Földi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能量自洽的自由空间有限近轴激光脉冲的量子光学表示方法，基于连续模式描述，构建了横向量子化矢量模式约化框架，使相干与非经典强场驱动光源能够统一处理，并提供测量脉冲与量子光学计算之间的便捷接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强场物理与量子光学交叉领域需要一种统一的理论框架来描述自由空间中的有限激光脉冲，既要能处理经典相干态脉冲（可仅由脉冲能量和分析信号确定量子态），又要能处理非经典脉冲（需要额外关联信息），同时避免引入人为的量子化体积，实现能量自洽的界面。

Method: 从平面波量子化出发，构建横向矢量模式约化，选定的模式可复现任意归一化空间-偏振分布。对相干脉冲，由测量脉冲能量和全实验室解析信号确定频率相关的相干态位移α(ω)；对非经典脉冲，通过引入正态和反常协方差核补充信息，并讨论基于g^(2)的压缩脉冲诊断；还推导了数值频域离散化的相位敏感收敛判据。

Result: 通过一个可精确求解的连续模式自由电子应用展示了：相干位移准确再现半经典平均轨迹，而场协方差决定电子波包展宽。证明了该框架能提供能量自洽的测量自由空间脉冲与量子光学计算之间的接口，且无需物理量子化体积。

Conclusion: 该框架为统一处理相干与非经典强场驱动光源提供了可行的理论途径，但在非经典情形下，仅有一阶场数据和总能量不足以唯一确定量子态，必须借助额外的关联信息或模型假设才能完成完整量子态重建。

Abstract: We formulate an energy-consistent quantum-optical representation of finite paraxial laser pulses in free space. The formulation is based on a continuous-mode description and provides a step toward a unified treatment of coherent and nonclassical strong-field drivers. Starting from plane-wave quantization, we construct a transverse vector-mode reduction in which the selected mode can reproduce an arbitrary normalized spatial--polarization profile. For a coherent pulse, the measured pulse energy and full laboratory analytic signal determine the frequency-dependent coherent-state displacement $(α(ω))$, without introducing a physical quantization volume. For nonclassical pulses, the same first-order field data and total energy do not uniquely specify the quantum state. Additional correlation information or model assumptions are required. We incorporate this information through normal and anomalous covariance kernels and discuss $g^{(2)}$-based diagnostics of squeezed pulses. We also derive a phase-sensitive convergence criterion for numerical frequency-bin discretizations. An exactly solvable continuum-mode free-electron application shows that the coherent displacement reproduces the semiclassical mean trajectory, whereas the field covariances determine the electron wave packet width. The framework offers a convenient, energy-consistent interface between measured free-space pulses and quantum-optical calculations.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum time-flip beats adaptive metrology: Asymptotic benefit, activation, and unsimulability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01175)
*Gaurang Agrawal,Pritam Halder,Aditi Sen De*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了具有不定时间方向的量子操作（时间翻转辅助策略）可以突破传统并行、自适应和因果叠加策略在量子计量学中的渐近等价性限制，实现超越标准海森堡极限的量子费希尔信息，提出"计量激活"现象，并确立不定时间方向作为量子计量的真正资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计量学中，自适应策略和因果叠加策略在有限次信道使用时优于并行方案，但其优势在渐近极限下消失。作者旨在寻找一种能够突破这种渐近等价性限制的策略，以实现在无限次使用时仍能保持甚至增强计量优势的目的。

Method: 作者提出采用时间翻转（time-flip）辅助策略（称为不定时间导向计量，ITDM），利用半定规划（SDP）进行严格分析，证明该策略可实现严格超过平行、自适应和因果叠加策略所能达到的最大量子费希尔信息（QFI）的值，并对特定Pauli噪声信道进行识别与分析。

Result: 研究表明，ITDM策略在有限和渐近多次信道使用中都能实现超越其他策略的QFI。对一类Pauli噪声信道，ITDM可实现海森堡标度（Heisenberg scaling），而所有平行、自适应和因果叠加策略只能实现标准标度（standard scaling），称之为"计量激活"现象。此外，该激活证明量子时间翻转和转置超映射无法被任何有限查询次数的常规量子电路或因果叠加策略模拟。

Conclusion: 不定时间方向是量子计量学中一种真正的、不可模拟的资源。该工作突破了传统量子计量策略的渐近等价性限制，揭示了时间翻转这一非因果资源在量子增强计量中的根本性作用。

Abstract: In quantum metrology, adaptive and causal-superposition strategies are proven to be beneficial over parallel schemes for a finite number of channel uses, but their advantages disappear in the asymptotic limit. We show that quantum operations with indefinite time direction, specifically, time-flip (TF)-assisted strategies, referred to as indefinite time-directed metrology (ITDM), can overcome this asymptotic equivalence. Using semidefinite programming, we rigorously demonstrate that TF-assisted protocols can achieve quantum Fisher information (QFI) strictly exceeding the maximum value attainable by parallel, adaptive, and causal-superposition strategies, both for finite and asymptotically many channel uses. Moreover, we identify a class of Pauli noise channels for which ITDM achieves Heisenberg scaling, while all parallel, adaptive, and causal-superposition strategies remain restricted to standard scaling. We call this phenomenon as metrological activation. Interestingly, this activation can be used to exhibit that the quantum time-flip and transposition supermaps cannot be simulated by conventional quantum circuits or causal-superposition strategies using any finite number of channel queries, thereby establishing indefinite time direction as a genuine resource for quantum metrology.

</details>


### [110] [Unbiased sampling from Boltzmann distributions with noisy energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01204)
*Iwo Sanderski,Gian Gentinetta,Giuseppe Carleo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种泊松乘积估计器（Poisson product estimator），用于在能量仅通过随机估计获得（如机器学习分子势、变分蒙特卡洛或量子计算机）时，无偏地从玻尔兹曼分布采样。该方法只需能量估计器的上界，无需噪声方差，且在高噪声下仍保持高效，解决了Ceperley-Dewing惩罚方法在噪声方差大时难以处理的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从玻尔兹曼分布采样是计算物理的核心问题，但当能量只能通过随机估计获得时（如机器学习势、变分蒙特卡洛、量子计算机），噪声会导致采样分布产生偏差。已有的Ceperley-Dewing惩罚方法虽然能纠正偏差，但需要知道噪声方差，且当噪声较大时计算上不可行。

Method: 提出Poisson product estimator，一种对玻尔兹曼权重wi的无偏且非负的估计器。它只需能量估计器的上界，不依赖噪声方差的精确知识，并且在高噪声条件下仍能保持计算效率。将该估计器用于梯度无关的变分量子电路优化中。

Result: 在最小基组下恢复了H₃⁺的基态能量；并且通过采样代替单轨迹跟随，成功绘制了变分能量景观（variational energy landscape）。

Conclusion: Poisson product estimator提供了一种在高噪声下仍然高效且无偏的玻尔兹曼权重估计方法，比传统惩罚方法具有更广泛的适用性，并已在变分量子电路优化中展示了实际有效性。

Abstract: Sampling from the Boltzmann distribution is central to computational physics, yet hard when the energy is known only through a stochastic estimate, such as with machine-learned molecular potentials, in variational Monte Carlo, or on quantum computers, because a noisy energy biases the sampled distribution. The penalty method of Ceperley and Dewing corrects this but requires the noise variance and becomes intractable when it is large. We introduce the Poisson product estimator, an unbiased, non-negative estimator of the Boltzmann weight that only needs an upper bound on the energy estimator and remains efficient at high noise. Using it to optimize a variational quantum circuit gradient-free, we recover the $H_3^+$ ground-state energy in a minimal basis and, by sampling rather than following a single trajectory, also map the variational energy landscape.

</details>


### [111] [Terrestrial Gravitational Wave Detection with Atom Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01227)
*Michael Werner,Ashkan Alibabaei,Naceur Gaaloul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析推导了地面原子干涉仪探测引力波的相位响应公式，发现相较于现有文献多出一个额外项并通过数值验证，同时优化了地面实验的几何参数，并使用开源Python算法对优化后的干涉仪进行了引力波探测方案数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子干涉仪是高精度惯性传感器，有望探测中频段引力波和暗物质候选粒子。虽然早先提出需在太空进行千米级基线实验，但近期发展表明地面百米级实验也可能探测引力波。然而地面实验的精确相位响应公式尚不完整，且针对优化后干涉仪的数值模拟方案在社区中缺失，这对准确建模噪声和非平凡引力背景至关重要。

Method: 作者解析推导了地面探测器对引力波的相位响应公式，识别出与现有文献相比的一个额外项并用数值方法验证；基于该公式分析地面原子干涉仪实验的最优几何参数；随后利用开源Python算法对优化后的干涉仪进行引力波探测方案的数值模拟。

Result: 推导出更为完整的地面原子干涉仪引力波相位响应解析公式（含额外项），并数值验证了其正确性；确定了地面实验的最优几何参数；通过开源Python算法成功实现了优化后干涉仪的引力波探测数值模拟。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了地面原子干涉仪引力波探测的理论框架，为社区提供了可用的数值模拟工具，有助于更准确地评估此类实验的噪声特性和探测灵敏度，推动地面中频段引力波探测的实际实施。

Abstract: Atom interferometers (AIFs) are highly precise inertial sensors and are considered promising instruments for the detection of gravitational waves (GWs) and certain dark matter (DM) candidates in the mid-frequency band. While GW detection with AIFs was initially proposed for space-based experiments with baselines spanning thousands of kilometers, recent developments suggest that terrestrial setups with baselines of at least 100 meters may also be capable of detecting GWs. In this work, we analytically derive the GW phase response formula of a ground detector, find an additional term compared to the existing literature and check it our findings numerically. Based on this treatment, we analyze the optimal geometric parameters for earth-bound AIF experiments. Subsequently, we numerically simulate GW detection schemes for these optimized interferometers using an open-source Python algorithm. Numerical simulations of these schemes have are not available to the community, yet crucial for the accurate modeling of noise and non-trivial gravitational backgrounds.

</details>


### [112] [A Backend-Agnostic MWIS Kernel for Stochastic Unit Commitment with Neutral-Atom Hardware Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01248)
*Jiying Chen,Min Lin,Jingwei Wen,Zhihong Zhang,Chuixiong Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后端无关的计算接口，将随机机组组合的离散决策层编译为基于移动的最大权重独立集（MWIS）问题，同时在经典计算层保留连续调度与可行性恢复。该方法在绿氢调度场景验证，并部署于QuEra Aquila中性原子量子处理器，成为首个将实际运营决策连接到可编程中性原子硬件的端到端工业调度工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件虽已开始处理结构化组合优化问题，但阻碍实际应用的两大障碍仍然存在：一是将真实运营模型映射到硬件兼容实例的困难，二是将含噪硬件输出转换回可行决策的挑战。

Method: 提出一种后端无关的计算接口，将随机机组组合的离散决策层编译为基于移动的MWIS问题，连续调度与可行性恢复保留在经典计算层。在50节点与144节点规模上进行验证，部署于QuEra Aquila中性原子量子处理器，并开展15天硬件运行实验。

Result: 在15天、50节点的硬件运行中，经经典精炼后的硬件生成解在每一天都达到或超过了精确MWIS获得的调度余量。扩展到144节点时，编码质量保持稳定，但完整原子阵列存活的概率（而非图嵌入）成为进一步扩展的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究成果建立了通向更大问题规模的硬件兼容计算路径，并为探索经典精确优化可能不再高效扩展的机制奠定基础。

Abstract: Quantum hardware is beginning to address structured combinatorial optimisation, but two steps still block practical use: mapping real operational models onto hardware-compatible instances, and converting noisy hardware output back into feasible decisions. Here we introduce a backend-agnostic computational interface that compiles the discrete decision layer of stochastic unit commitment into a move-based maximum-weight independent set (MWIS) problem, while retaining continuous dispatch and feasibility recovery in the classical computational layer. We validate the approach in a green hydrogen scheduling setting and deploy it on the QuEra Aquila neutral-atom quantum processor. This is the first end-to-end industrial scheduling workflow that connects real operational decisions to programmable neutral-atom hardware through a solver-agnostic MWIS representation. Across a 15-day hardware campaign on 50-node instances, hardware-generated solutions after classical refinement match or exceed the dispatch margins obtained from exact MWIS on every day. When scaling to 144 nodes, encoding quality remains stable, while the probability that the full atom array survives, rather than graph embedding, emerges as the dominant bottleneck to further scaling. Together, these results establish a hardware-compatible computational pathway toward larger problem scales, and lay the groundwork for exploring regimes in which exact classical optimisation may no longer scale efficiently.

</details>


### [113] [DF-SQD: Deterministic Fields for Sampling-Based Quantum Diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01264)
*Kushagra Agarwal,Anupama Ray*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-SQD，一种混合量子计算算法，从双电子张量的选定双因子分解叶中推导确定性辅助场电路，用于采样占据数构型，并在经典侧评估哈密顿量。在N2和铁硫簇活性空间上，DF-SQD相比标准SQD以更浅电路、更少采样次数实现了更高的能量精度和更小的子空间维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子器件中，算法需要在浅电路和误差缓解支持下发现更好结果，同时要有收敛证明或可信度解释方法。基于采样的量子对角化方法(SQD)依赖量子计算平台采样比特串用于哈密顿量投影，但现有方法在采样效率和电路深度方面存在局限，需要更高效的确定性方案。

Method: DF-SQD是一种混合算法，从两电子张量的选定双因子分解(DF)叶中推导出确定性辅助场电路。这些电路提出占据数构型，随后通过选定的构型相互作用方法(SCI)评估原始活性空间哈密顿量，并可重新校准后续提议轮次。该方法结合了确定性辅助场电路与经典选态CI，形成闭环优化。

Result: 在N2（32量子比特，6-31G基组）上，DF-SQD精度提高了45倍，子空间缩小11.23%，在量子设备上比SQD快2.93倍。在40比特[Fe2S2(SCH3)4]2-铁硫簇上，DF-SQD用40万shots生成2.21亿维子空间，而SQD需150万shots生成2.38亿维子空间，采样量减少3.75倍；在匹配的5000万子空间维度下，DF-SQD精度提升1.32倍，相对误差降低24.5%。

Conclusion: DF-SQD方法能够以更浅的电路发现更好结果，具备采样效率优势，通过构型恢复实现针对性误差缓解，并具有经验性收敛观测，是量子中心超算平台上有效的量子对角化方法。

Abstract: Sampling-based quantum diagonalization method exploits Quantum-centric supercomputing platforms to sample bitstrings for Hamiltonian projection on a quantum computer, and then classically diagonalize the Hamiltonian to estimate the eigenvalues and eigenvectors. In current quantum devices an algorithm is useful when shallow quantum circuits with error mitigation support can discover better results while having either a proof of convergence or some method to explain trust in experiment. In this paper, we introduce DF-SQD, a hybrid algorithm that derives deterministic auxiliary-field circuits from selected double-factorization leaves of the two-electron tensor. The circuits propose occupation-number configurations, while selected configuration interaction evaluates the original active-space Hamiltonian and can recentre subsequent proposal rounds. On N2 (32 qubits; 6-31G basis) and a 40-qubit [Fe2S2(SCH3)4]2- active-space Hamiltonian, we show that DF-SQD improves the energy obtained from sampled determinant spaces while using shallow number-preserving circuits in both simulator and hardware runs. For N2, DF-SQD is 45x more accurate with a 11.23\% smaller subspace, and due to its ability to sample better bitstrings at lesser shots it is 2.93x faster than SQD in quantum devices. For the iron-sulfur cluster, DF-SQD generated a subspace dimension of 221M with 400K shots, while SQD needed 1.5M shots to generate a 238M subspace, thus we have better subspace recovery evident from the hardware at 3.75x reduced shots. At a matched 50M subspace dimension, DF-SQD is 1.32x more accurate (achieves a 24.5\% relative error reduction over standard SQD). So overall, our method is able to discover better results with shallower circuits, is sample efficient, uses configuration recovery (so has targeted error mitigation) and we have empirical convergence observation.

</details>


### [114] [Dynamical regimes of QAOA gradient response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01280)
*Zarin Shakibaei,Alexander Schnell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于范数加权的层强度和代价-混和器不平衡度的动力学表示方法来描述QAOA参数空间，将演化的整体尺度与两个生成器的相对贡献分离。通过MaxCut的精确态模拟，发现梯度景观在动力学变量上具有跨电路深度和调度结构持久存在的粗粒度组织，而近优解位于一个独特的中间动力学区域而非最大局部梯度处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生QAOA角度参数化无法区分特定参数特征与底层电路动力学的更广泛变化，使得难以刻画梯度景观如何随电路参数和问题规模变化，需要一种能分离尺度效应与生成器相对贡献的动力学框架来理解QAOA的可训练性。

Method: 引入两种动力学变量：(1) 基于范数加权的层强度（norm-weighted layer strength），表征演化的整体尺度；(2) 代价-混和器不平衡度（cost-mixer imbalance），表征两个生成器的相对贡献。使用MaxCut问题的精确态模拟，在不同电路深度和调度结构下分析梯度景观在动力学变量中的组织模式，并对比均匀与非均匀调度下的行为差异。

Result: 梯度景观在动力学变量中呈现粗粒度组织结构，该结构跨电路深度和调度结构持续存在，而精细干涉图样仍依赖于具体调度。近优解不与最大局部梯度重合，而是位于独特的中间动力学区域。均匀调度能恢复该区域的大致位置，非均匀调度主要重组其精细结构。在动力学表示中近优解区域保持延伸，但其在原生QAOA角度中的原像在大规模系统下显著压缩。

Conclusion: 近优QAOA动力学的持久性与其在原生参数化中的可达性是分离的。该动力学框架为跨电路与问题尺度解释QAOA可训练性提供了新视角，表明原生参数空间中的压缩并非反映动力学本身的失效，而是参数化方式对有用动力学区域可达性的影响。

Abstract: Characterizing the trainability of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) requires understanding how its gradient landscape changes across circuit parameters and problem size. Yet these gradients are usually described in terms of the native QAOA angles, making it difficult to distinguish parameter specific features from broader changes in the underlying circuit dynamics. Here we introduce a dynamical representation of the QAOA parameter space based on a norm-weighted layer strength and a cost--mixer imbalance, separating the overall scale of the evolution from the relative contribution of the two generators.
  Using exact-state simulations of MaxCut, we find that the gradient landscape exhibits a coarse organization in these dynamical variables that persists across changes in circuit depth and schedule structure, while the finer interference pattern remains schedule dependent. Near-optimal solutions do not simply coincide with the largest local gradients, but instead occupy a distinct intermediate dynamical regime. Uniform schedules recover the broad location of this regime, whereas nonuniform schedules mainly reorganize its fine structure. Across the system sizes studied, near-optimal solution regions remain extended in the dynamical representation while their preimages in the native QAOA angles become substantially compressed at larger sizes. These results separate the persistence of useful QAOA dynamics from their accessibility in the native parameterization, and provide a dynamical framework for interpreting QAOA trainability across circuit and problem scales.

</details>


### [115] [A Scalable Multi-Protocol Platform for Quantum Key Distribution Simulation with Rigorous Statistical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01297)
*Anuj Rathore,Kartick Sutradhar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的QKD仿真平台，在单一Python/Qiskit引擎中实现了BB84、B92、E91和BBM92四种底层量子密钥分发协议，并集成了光纤衰减、信源/探测器损耗、偏振漂移和可配置的中继窃听等共享损伤模型。平台提供桌面应用和Web客户端两种接口，所有实验结果均来自20次独立重复试验（每次10000量子比特），报告均值、标准差和95%置信区间。在25公里光纤链路上BB84获得最高平均密钥率160,045 Hz，E91协议下CHSH S统计量在基线时为2.12，激活窃听后降至1.58，展示了独立于QBER的贝尔不等式入侵检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD提供基于量子物理的信息论安全密钥建立，但其实际应用受限于光子硬件的高昂成本和现有仿真工具的碎片化。现有模拟器大多只支持单一协议且仅报告单次随机运行结果，难以进行系统性协议对比和可复现的统计推断。因此需要建立一个统一的多协议仿真平台来克服这些局限。

Method: 该平台在统一的Python/Qiskit引擎中实现了四种基础QKD协议（BB84、B92、E91、BBM92），共享包含光纤衰减、信源和探测器损耗、偏振漂移及可配置中继窃听的损伤模型。平台通过两个独立界面访问同一后端：基于Tkinter和Matplotlib的桌面应用用于本地实验，基于React和Node.js/Express的浏览器Web客户端提供免安装远程访问。所有结果均来自20次独立运行、每次10000量子比特的重复实验研究，并全程报告均值、标准差和95%置信区间。

Result: 在25公里光纤链路上，BB84获得最高平均密钥率160,045 Hz，其次是BBM92（80,023 Hz）、E91（52,815 Hz）和B92（40,011 Hz），排序与仿真推导的筛选效率精确吻合。E91协议下，CHSH S统计量基线均值为2.12，窃听者激活时降至1.58，成功展示了独立于QBER的贝尔不等式入侵检测能力。

Conclusion: 该统一QKD仿真平台通过多协议集成、共享损伤建模、双接口访问和严格的重复运行统计方法，有效解决了QKD仿真工具碎片化的问题，为系统性协议对比和可复现的统计推断提供了可靠基础设施，并验证了贝尔不等式在窃听检测中的实用性。

Abstract: Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) offers information- theoretically secure key establishment grounded in the laws of quantum physics, yet its practical reach is limited by the prohibitive cost of photonic hardware and the fragmented nature of existing simulation tools. Most simulators support only a single protocol and report results from individual stochastic runs, making systematic protocol comparison and reproducible statistical inference difficult. This paper presents a unified QKD simulation platform that implements four foundational protocols BB84, B92, E91, and BBM92 within a single Python/Qiskit engine. A shared impairment model covers fiber attenuation, source and detector losses, po- larization drift, and configurable intercept-resend eavesdropping. The platform is accessible through two independent interfaces that share the same backend: a desktop application (Tkinter, Matplotlib) for local experimentation and a browser-based web client (React, Node.js/Express) for zero-install remote access. All reported results are drawn from repeated-run studies (20 independent runs, 10000 qubits each), with mean, standard deviation, and 95% confidence intervals stated throughout. At a 25 km fiber link, BB84 achieves the highest mean key-rate of 160,045 Hz, followed by BBM92 (80023 Hz), E91 (52815 Hz), and B92 (40011 Hz) ordering that tracks simulation-derived sifting efficiencies precisely. Under the E91 protocol, the CHSH S-statistic averages 2.12 at baseline and falls to 1.58 when an eavesdropper is activated, demonstrating Bell-inequality-based intrusion detection independent of QBER

</details>


### [116] [Emerging Personas and Tools for Hybrid Quantum-HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01299)
*Eun-Kyung Lee,Jessie Yu,Claudio Carvalho,Yoonho Park,Marcelo Amaral,Tim Osborne,Woong Shin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别并刻画了量子中心超级计算（QCSC）环境中六种不同的用户画像（personas），分析了它们在量子与HPC系统交界处的职责、工具及对运行数据的不同需求，为构建统一数据表示与分析框架奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子处理器（QPU）与高性能计算（HPC）系统深度融合形成量子中心超级计算（QCSC）范式，该领域出现了新的运维、软件和基础设施挑战。然而，不同参与者在QCSC环境中扮演的角色及其对运行数据的差异化需求尚未被系统界定，缺乏统一的框架来支撑多样化的用户群体。

Method: 本研究通过识别和刻画QCSC环境中六种不同用户画像，特别关注量子与HPC系统交界处涌现的新兴角色，系统分析每种画像的职责、所用工具以及它们对运行数据的不同要求。

Result: 识别出六种QCSC用户画像，厘清了新兴角色的职责边界与工具集，揭示了各画像对运行数据需求的差异性，并指出需要一致的数据表示与分析框架来支持量子-HPC生态中的多样化用户。

Conclusion: 通过定义用户画像及其数据需求，为量子-HPC生态系统中数据表示与分析框架的后续讨论和建设提供了基础性参考框架。

Abstract: Quantum computing is entering an era where quantum processing units (QPUs) with tens to hundreds of qubits are increasingly integrated with high-performance computing (HPC) systems to support hybrid quantum-classical workloads. This paradigm, often referred to as Quantum-Centric SuperComputing (QCSC), introduces new operational, software, and infrastructure challenges. In this paper, we identify and characterize six distinct personas involved in QCSC environments. Particular attention is given to emerging roles that arise at the interface between quantum and HPC systems. We analyze the responsibilities and tools associated with each persona and examine their differing requirements for operational data. Consistent data representations and analytics frameworks will be required to support diverse users across the quantum-HPC ecosystem. By defining personas and their data needs, we provide a foundation for future discussions in this critical area.

</details>


### [117] [Completing or Refusing Low-Dimensional Records of Structured Quantum Circuits: Measurement Loss, Compression Loss, and Hardware Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01303)
*Gunhee Cho,Juhee Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无须硬件噪声模型或低维统计族的框架，用于评估结构化量子电路通过低维记录（records）压缩原始测量结果时的信息损失。引入Hellinger残差及局部二次项（条件协方差），并结合量子Fisher信息与Fisher度量，给出同时置信界以支持批准、拒绝或延迟决策。证明有限库完备性定理：可执行扩充要么终止于保留所有声明响应的记录，要么证明无法通过扩充消除损失。通过积分闭包与Rees赋值刻画解析控制胚沿所有解析控制弧的性质。在三比特计算中分离测量损失与记录损失，并在IBM Kingston/Marrakesh及IQM Garnet实验中验证，包括校准测试次数减少33.3%、记录级漂移仅为全分布漂移0.0797倍等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化量子电路常被低维记录概括，但记录在合并响应随电路参数变化不同的结果时会丢失控制依赖信息。现有方法缺乏无需参数化硬件噪声模型或低维统计族假设的评估手段，因此需要一种可直接度量该信息损失的量子信息几何框架。

Method: 利用量子Fisher信息界预测量灵敏度，通过测量与记录的Fisher度量描述设备可达灵敏度；定义Hellinger残差度量记录移除的响应量，其局部二次项为全结局得分之条件协方差；构造同时置信界完成批准/拒绝/延迟决策。理论层面证明有限库完备性定理及积分闭包与Rees估值对解析控制胚中响应保持的表征。实验采用三比特分离测量损失与记录损失，并在IBM与IQM平台上进行消融与端到端验证。

Result: 三比特计算有效分离测量损失与记录损失；IBM Kingston端到端实验将校准测量次数减少33.3%并满足预设非劣效标准；IQM Garnet双epoch数据显示记录级漂移中位数为全分布漂移的0.0797倍，但35/36设置中存在显著残余漂移；负对照实验有效。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在无需硬件噪声参数模型的条件下，系统地检测量子电路压缩记录的控制依赖信息丢失，并给出可执行的批准、拒绝或延迟决策。实验验证了故障检测的有效性，但未声称普适压缩性能或设备量子Fisher信息的普适结论。

Abstract: Structured quantum circuits are often summarized by low-dimensional records. Records lose control-dependent information when they merge outcomes whose probabilities respond differently to circuit parameters. We assess this loss without a parametric hardware-noise model or low-dimensional statistical family. Quantum Fisher information bounds premeasurement sensitivity; Fisher metrics of measurements and records describe device-attainable sensitivity. A Hellinger residual measures the response removed by a record, while its local quadratic term is the conditional covariance of the full-outcome score. Simultaneous confidence bounds support approval, refusal, or deferral.
  We prove a finite-library completion theorem: executable augmentations terminate with either a record preserving every declared response or proof that no library augmentation removes the loss. For an analytic control germ, integral closures characterize preservation along every analytic control arc, and finitely many Rees valuations detect failure. This state--measurement--record chain connects an all-arc criterion to executable completion or refusal, an attainable-Fisher local kernel criterion, and finite-sample decisions.
  A three-qubit calculation separates measurement loss from record loss. IBM experiments on Kingston and Marrakesh test decisions in fixed-particle-number and GHZ families, including negative controls. An end-to-end Kingston experiment reduces calibration shots by 33.3% while meeting prespecified noninferiority criteria on two held-out objectives. In two-epoch IQM Garnet data, median record-level drift is 0.0797 times full-distribution drift, but a significant residual remains in 35 of 36 settings. These experiments validate failure detection on the tested circuits; they do not establish universal compression performance or device quantum Fisher information.

</details>

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需微波的、超快且高保真度的超导量子比特态制备与读取方法，仅需10纳秒即可实现>99%的保真度，并通过量子磁通参量放大实现15纳秒的量子-数字全转换，比现有微波技术快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子比特的态制备与测量是实现量子计算系统的关键环节。现有最先进方法依赖精细调谐的微波信号且需要约100纳秒的测量时间，严重制约了超导量子计算机的可扩展性与性能表现。

Method: 作者提出并演示了无需微波的新型超导量子比特制备与读取方法，利用量子磁通参量放大器（quantum flux parametrons）实现放大，该方案与可扩展的超导数字控制系统兼容，可在极短时间内完成量子到数字的完整转换。

Result: 制备和读取操作均可在仅10纳秒内实现超过99%的保真度，同时保持量子比特相干性。利用量子磁通参量放大实现了仅需15纳秒的完整量子-数字转换，比现有最先进的基于微波的技术快一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该微波自由技术为超导量子计算的规模化提供了一条可行的路径，显著提升态制备与测量的速度和保真度，同时简化了对精细微波信号调控的需求，有望推动可扩展超导数字控制系统的实际应用。

Abstract: Fast, high-fidelity, scalable state preparation and measurement is critical to the realization of a quantum computing system. The state-of-the-art methods for preparation and readout of superconducting qubits require finely tuned microwave signals and ~100 ns of measurement time, which are major obstacles to the scalability and performance of superconducting quantum computers. Here, we have demonstrated novel, microwave-free methods for both preparation and readout of superconducting qubits with >99% fidelity in only 10 ns for either operation while maintaining qubit coherence. This technology is compatible with scalable superconducting digital control systems, and using quantum flux parametrons for amplification, we demonstrated full quantum-to-digital conversion in only 15 ns, which is an order of magnitude faster than state-of-the-art microwave-based techniques.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum phase transitions of the Cavity Heisenberg spin-chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01340)
*Lv-Ting Gong,Shao-Fan He,Fu-Quan Dou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了腔海森堡自旋链模型中的基态量子相变(QPTs)、激发态量子相变(ESQPTs)以及遍历-非遍历转变(ENET)。通过结合量子信息度量与半经典固定点分析，作者识别出一个在正常相和超辐射相之间插值的变形相，其特征是态密度中的对数非解析性以及两个额外的跳跃不连续。研究还通过能级统计、参与率和多重分形分析阐明了ESQPT与ENET之间联系的谱结构机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子临界性与遍历性破缺之间的相互作用是复杂量子多体系统中的一个核心挑战。理解QPTs和ESQPTs与遍历-非遍历转变之间的内在联系，对于建立描述光-物质量子多体系统中相结构和谱特征的统一框架至关重要。

Method: 采用量子信息度量（如纠缠、保真度等）与半经典固定点分析相结合的方法，研究腔海森堡自旋链模型。利用能级统计、参与比率和多重分形分析来表征谱结构，揭示ESQPT与ergodic-nonergodic转变(ENET)之间的联系。

Result: 发现了一个位于正常相和超辐射相之间的变形相，该相在态密度(DoS)中表现出对数非解析性以及两个额外的跳跃不连续性。通过能级统计、参与率和多重分形分析阐明了ESQPT与ENET之间联系的谱结构机制。

Conclusion: 该工作为表征光-物质量子多体系统中的相结构和谱特征提供了一个通用框架，深化了对量子临界性、ESQPTs与遍历性破缺之间相互作用的物理理解。

Abstract: The interplay between quantum criticality and ergodicity breaking constitutes a central challenge in complex quantum many-body systems. Here, we investigate ground-state quantum phase transitions (QPTs) and excited-state quantum phase transitions (ESQPTs), as well as ergodic-nonergodic transition in the cavity Heisenberg spin-chain (CHS) model. By combining quantum information measures with semiclassical fixed-point analysis, we identify a deformed phase that interpolates between the normal and superradiant phases, featuring a logarithmic nonanalyticity in the density of states (DoS) and two additional jump discontinuities. We further elucidate the spectral-structure mechanism underlying the connection between ESQPTs and the ergodic--nonergodic transition (ENET) via level statistics, participation ratios, and multifractal analysis. Our results provide a general framework for characterizing phase structures and spectral features in light--matter quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [120] [Local Gaussian bounds on the non-destructive discrimination of two-mode squeezed states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01373)
*Mi-Jung So,James Moran,Youngrong Lim,Mahn-Soo Choi,Hyukjoon Kwon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了两个双模压缩真空态的局域高斯测量非破坏性判别问题，研究了判别成功概率与保真度之间的权衡关系，并构建了数值最优的局域高斯测量协议，同时扩展到预共享纠缠情形以突破局域高斯界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型量子测量是破坏性的，状态在测量后不可逆转地改变。作者希望将有限维量子系统中已建立的信息获取与扰动之间的权衡关系，自然地扩展到无限维连续变量系统，研究非破坏性判别两个双模压缩真空态的可能性及其边界。

Method: 作者分析使用局域高斯测量对两个双模压缩真空态进行非破坏性判别，研究判别成功概率与结果态对初始态保真度之间的权衡关系，构建了局域高斯测量协议并在数值探索的类别中证明其最优性；进一步允许预共享纠缠，展示该机制可超越标准局域高斯界限。

Result: 构建了数值最优的局域高斯测量协议，揭示了成功概率与保真度之间的权衡曲线；当引入额外的预共享纠缠后，该权衡关系可以超越标准局域高斯测量下所能达到的界限。

Conclusion: 该工作将有限维量子系统中纠缠态判别的信息获取-扰动权衡关系自然推广到无限维连续变量系统，为连续变量量子信息处理中的非破坏性测量提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Typical measurement setups in quantum systems are destructive, meaning that states are irretrievably altered after measurement. In this work, we analyse non-destructive discrimination of two two-mode squeezed vacuum states using local Gaussian measurements. We investigate a tradeoff relation between the success probability of discrimination and the fidelity of the resulting state with the initial state, and construct a protocol given by local Gaussian measurements, which is optimal within our numerically explored class. We also extend to the case where we allow for additional pre-shared entanglement, and show that this regime allows us to exceed the standard local Gaussian bound for the fidelity-success probability tradeoff. Our work provides a natural extension of the tradeoff between information gain and disturbance in entangled-state discrimination, previously established for finite-dimensional quantum systems, to infinite-dimensional continuous-variable systems.

</details>


### [121] [Exact Virtual Channel Programming with Vanishing Excess Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01419)
*Mingrui Jing,Mengbo Guo,Hongshun Yao,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子程序对物理处理器重构酉信道的基本限制，建立了"以量子程序存储目标酉信道、通过采样物理信道并经典后处理来重构其输出可观测量"这一范式下的定量下界与最优协议。作者证明了任意d维信道下最优单份采样开销与系统维度呈二次方增长，并给出了固定维度下多余的采样开销随相同Choi程序副本数倒数线性消失的精确定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维物理处理器无法精确编程连续族的酉信道，这是一个已知的定性障碍。本文的动机是将这一"无编程障碍"转化为定量结果——理解当目标信道存储于归一化Choi态时，通过采样物理信道和经典后处理重构其输出可观测量所需的最小采样开销，从而揭示量子程序记忆与经典采样之间的基本权衡。

Method: 作者构造了一个与目标无关的精确重构协议，上界结合了确定性端到端隐形传态（port-based teleportation）与一种纠正其去极化畸变的拟分解方法；下界方面，将任何低开销重构协议映射为对未知酉矩阵的物理学习器，并利用局部量子估计恢复相同的领先系数。

Result: 证明了相应问题中任意d维信道的最优单份采样开销，其随系统维度二次增长；进一步证明了固定维度的尖锐定律：多余开销随相同Choi程序副本数N以1/N的逆比例关系消失。领先系数反映了局部可学习的酉自由度。

Conclusion: 本文将通用的无编程障碍重新表述为量子程序记忆与经典采样之间的定量权衡，确立了最优采样开销的精确增长律与有限副本修正律，表明其领先成本由酉信道的局域可学习自由度决定。

Abstract: A finite-dimensional physical processor cannot exactly program a continuous family of distinct unitary channels. We show that this obstruction becomes quantitative when the target channel is stored in a normalized Choi state and its output observables are reconstructed by sampling physical channels and classically post-processing their measurement outcomes. For arbitrary $d$-dimensional channels, we construct a target-independent exact reconstruction protocol and prove the optimal one-copy sampling overhead, which grows quadratically with system dimension. We further prove the sharp fixed-$d$ law that the excess overhead vanishes inversely with the number of identical Choi programs. The upper bound combines deterministic port-based teleportation with a quasi-decomposition that corrects its depolarizing distortion. The converse maps any low-overhead reconstruction protocol to a physical learner of unknown unitaries and uses local quantum estimation to recover the same leading coefficient. These results recast the universal no-programming obstruction as a quantitative trade-off between quantum program memory and classical sampling, with a leading cost that reflects the locally learnable unitary degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [122] [Verifiable quantum advantage in extremely low depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01448)
*Alexandru Gheorghiu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可由浅层量子电路解决的采样问题：该问题在基于格（lattice）的假设下对经典多项式时间算法是困难的，但可由经典计算机高效验证。该量子采样器有两种实现：一种使用对数-对数深度（QNC^0[log log]）电路，另一种使用常数深度且具有无界扇入门（QAC^0）的电路。该构造本质上是将Arabadjieva等人（2025）基于LWE的单轮量子性证明编译到极低深度，代价是依赖更强的假设（包括自适应硬核比特性质的强化变体）。与以往低深度量子性证明不同，该方案无需中途测量或前馈，仅需运行浅层电路并采样输出分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索极低深度（浅层）量子电路是否具有足够的结构来解决某些经典上困难但可高效验证的任务，特别是希望构造不依赖中途测量和前馈的低深度量子性证明（proof of quantumness），并为无条件区分浅层量子与经典计算复杂性类提供新的证据和工具。

Method: 作者将Arabadjieva等人（2025）基于LWE的单轮量子性证明协议编译为极浅层量子电路。具体技术手段包括：(1) 利用QNC^0[log log]电路（对数-对数深度的单/双比特门电路）和QAC^0电路（常数深度、无界扇入门电路）实现量子采样器；(2) 在原有格知识假设（lattice knowledge assumption）之外，引入LWE的自适应硬核比特性质的强化变体假设（并提供了支持性证据）。整个量子计算只需运行一个浅层电路并从其输出分布中采样，不需要中途测量或经典前馈。

Result: 成功构造了一个采样问题，其可被QNC^0[log log]或QAC^0类浅层量子电路解决，在基于LWE的假设下对经典多项式时间算法困难，且可由经典计算机高效验证。证明了浅层量子电路能够解决某些经典上困难且可验证的任务，无需中途测量和前馈，比以往的低深度量子性证明更简洁。

Conclusion: 该工作表明极浅层量子电路（甚至常数深度的QAC^0）具有足够丰富的结构来完成经典上困难的任务。代价是引入了较非标准但合理的密码学假设（自适应硬核比特性质的强化变体）。这推进了浅层量子计算的证明能力研究，为量子优势的低深度化提供了新的构造路径和理论保证。

Abstract: We give a sampling problem that is solvable by shallow quantum circuits, hard for polynomial-time classical algorithms under lattice-based assumptions, and efficiently verifiable by a classical computer. The quantum sampler admits two implementations: one uses log-logarithmic-depth quantum circuits with one- and two-qubit gates, i.e., $\mathsf{QNC}^0[\log\log]$ circuits, while the other uses constant-depth quantum circuits with unbounded fan-in gates, i.e., $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuits. Our construction can be seen as compiling the Learning with Errors (LWE)-based single-round proof of quantumness of Arabadjieva et al. (2025) to very low depth. The price paid for this compilation is the reliance on less standard, though well-motivated, assumptions: in addition to the lattice knowledge assumption used by Arabadjieva et al. (2025), we require a strengthened variant of the adaptive-hardcore-bit property of LWE, for which we provide supporting evidence. Unlike previous low-depth proofs of quantumness, the quantum computation here requires no mid-circuit measurements or feed-forward: it consists only of running a shallow circuit and sampling from its output distribution. This shows that shallow quantum circuits have sufficient structure to solve certain classically hard tasks whose solutions can be verified efficiently.

</details>


### [123] [Quantum Weighted Moving Average for Predicting Limit Order Book Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01524)
*Matthias Kamm,Dinh-Long Vu,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子计算机在多元金融时间序列预测中的应用，提出了量子加权移动平均（QWMA）模型，用于从限价订单簿数据预测价格趋势。模型结合经典数据预处理与基于线性组合酉算子的量子层，在FI-2010基准数据集和中国A股数据集上评估，性能接近最优经典模型，但未展示量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算能否有效应用于多元金融时间序列预测这一实际问题。具体而言，作者希望从限价订单簿（LOB）数据中预测价格趋势，并检验量子模型在真实金融数据上的可行性。

Method: 首先识别经典模型的关键组成部分，据此提出量子加权移动平均（QWMA）模型。该模型包含两个核心模块：(1) 经典预处理阶段，对特征维度和时间维度分别进行归一化；(2) 基于线性组合酉算子（LCU）的层，用于处理以酉方式嵌入的经典数据。此外还考虑了QWMA的若干特化变体，包括与广泛使用的指数移动平均（EMA）相关的变体。

Result: 模型在FI-2010基准数据集和另一组中国A股数据集上进行评估。虽然没有证据表明量子优势，但经典-量子混合模型的性能接近最优经典模型。量子部分具有足够的表达能力，能够聚焦于时间序列中最具预测性的部分。

Conclusion: 量子加权移动平均模型在金融时序预测中是可行的，但当前尚未实现超越经典模型的量子优势。作者同时指出了方法本身的局限性以及所用数据集存在的限制，为后续量子金融机器学习研究提供了参考框架。

Abstract: Can quantum computers be useful for forecasting multivariate financial time series? In this work, we consider the problem of predicting price trends from limit order book (LOB) data. After identifying key components of classical models, we introduce the quantum weighted moving average (QWMA) model. The two main building blocks are, first, classically preprocessing via normalization of both feature and temporal dimensions and, second, a linear combination of unitaries-based layer for unitarily-embedded classical data. The models are evaluated on the FI-2010 benchmark dataset and a second dataset of China A-share stocks. While we do not present evidence of quantum advantage, the combined classical-quantum model demonstrates performances close to the best classical models. The quantum part of the model is expressive enough to focus on the most predictive parts of the time series. Several specializations of the QWMA model are considered, in particular a variant related to the widely-used exponential moving average (EMA). We give consideration to the limitations of the methods and the datasets.

</details>
