### [161] [Guiding center quantization of a quantum Hall analog of Hawking radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00158)
*Rodrigo Andrade e Silva,Ted Jacobson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了量子霍尔效应中的类霍金辐射，证明其可通过经典引导中心理论的量子化精确描述。研究发现这种辐射实质上是类安鲁辐射，其热谱源于非对易平面上的运动学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新探究量子霍尔系统中类霍金辐射的物理本质，澄清其与引导中心理论、非对易几何以及类安鲁辐射之间的关系，为深入理解这一模拟引力效应提供更清晰的物理图像。

Method: 采用经典引导中心理论的量子化方法，将电子的回旋运动粗粒化，仅保留漂移运动。研究发现在非对易平面上，半平面内局域化的电子状态必须沿边界方向全域支撑，这一运动学约束直接导致沿边界双向传播的辐射。通过论证实验室时间与模拟闵可夫斯基时空中的boost角成正比，将辐射识别为类安鲁辐射。

Result: 证明了量子霍尔系统的类霍金辐射可以由引导中心理论的量子化精确描述；该辐射沿边界从原点向两侧双向传播，且相对于实验室时间呈现热谱。辐射的热特性源于模拟闵可夫斯基时空中类安鲁效应。

Conclusion: 量子霍尔系统中的辐射并非传统意义上的类霍金辐射，而是类安鲁辐射。这一重新诠释为模拟引力系统中的量子效应提供了更深层的物理理解，凸显了非对易几何在其中的关键作用。

Abstract: We revisit the quantum Hall analog of Hawking radiation in which a Fermi sea of electrons occupying half of a plane, subjected to a quadrupolar electric potential, gives rise to analog Hawking radiation of chiral edge modes. We show that the phenomenon is accurately captured by quantization of the classical guiding center theory, where the gyroscopic motion of the electrons is coarse grained and only the drift motion is resolved. The quantum dynamics takes place on a non-commutative plane, which requires an electron localized in the half-plane to be supported everywhere along the edge direction. This kinematical constraint on the quantum state leads directly to the radiation, which propagates in both directions away from the origin along the edge. The radiation is thermal with respect to laboratory time, which is equal (up to a constant factor) to the boost angle in the analog Minkowski spacetime in which the chiral edge modes propagate, so in fact it corresponds to analog Unruh radiation.

</details>


### [162] [Gravitational waves from core collapse of rotating very-massive stars: 3D numerical relativity computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00200)
*Alan Tsz-Lok Lam,Masaru Shibata,Sho Fujibayashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过轴对称和三维数值相对论模拟，研究了约200、300、500和1100倍太阳质量的高速旋转超重大质量恒星核心坍缩成黑洞的过程。发现当黑洞的无量纲自旋超过0.8时，会稳定形成质量超过黑洞质量10%的巨大致密吸积盘，这些盘易发生非轴对称变形，触发频率约为10-50 Hz的引力波爆发，可被爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探索者探测器探测到，甚至可观测到数Gpc之外的源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超重大质量恒星核心坍缩形成黑洞过程中的引力波信号特征，特别是旋转对吸积盘形成及非轴对称不稳定性引发的引力波爆发的触发条件，以评估下一代引力波探测器对这类源的探测能力。

Method: 采用轴对称和三维（3D）数值相对论方法，对质量约为200、300、500和1100倍太阳质量的旋转超重大质量恒星核心坍缩为黑洞的过程进行数值模拟。

Result: 当形成的黑洞无量纲自旋参数超过0.8时，黑洞周围一致地形成巨大的致密吸积盘。这些吸积盘携带超过黑洞质量10%的质量，容易发生非轴对称变形，触发频率约为10-50 Hz的引力波爆发，此类信号可被爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探索者探测到，甚至能探测到几Gpc之外的源。轴对称坍缩产生的引力波信号振幅较低、频率较高。

Conclusion: 黑洞自旋是决定是否形成大规模吸积盘及后续引力波爆发的关键参数；非轴对称盘不稳定性产生的低频引力波信号是未来三代引力波探测器的重要探测目标，可作为超重大质量恒星黑洞形成事件的观测特征。

Abstract: We numerically study the collapse of rotating, very-massive stellar cores with masses of $\approx 200$, $300$, $500$, and $1100M_\odot$ into black holes using both axisymmetric and three-dimensional (3D) numerical relativity. Our results indicate that when the dimensionless spin of the resulting black hole exceeds 0.8, a massive disk consistently forms around it. These massive, compact disks, carrying more than about 10\% of the black hole's mass, are prone to non-axisymmetric deformations that trigger gravitational-wave bursts with frequencies around 10-50 Hz. Such waves could be detected by the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer, even from sources a few Gpc away. We also summarize the gravitational-wave signals from axisymmetric collapse, which tend to have lower amplitudes and higher frequencies than those caused by non-axisymmetric instabilities.

</details>


### [163] [Unimodular Gravity with Arbitrary Diffusion Function: A Dynamical System Reconstruction Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00201)
*Gabriel Gómez,Guillermo Palma,Norman Cruz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在动态系统重构框架下研究了单模引力中的宇宙学扩散模型。通过将扩散扇区的对数斜率视为可逆动力学变量，从相空间结构中系统重构扩散函数，识别出物理上可接受的固定点，发现了与幂律扩散扇区相关的物质-扩散缩放解，以及无需显式宇宙学常数即可驱动晚期加速膨胀的纯扩散主导构型。进一步将局部行为推广到全局宇宙学演化，开发了基于扩散斜率动态演化的重构形式体系，允许轨迹在宇宙历史中在不同扩散区域间插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一种系统框架，能够直接从相空间动力学中构造和分类单模引力中可行的扩散宇宙学模型，避免依赖显式宇宙学常数解释晚期宇宙加速膨胀。

Method: 采用动态系统重构方法，将扩散扇区的对数斜率作为可逆动力学变量，从宇宙学演化的相空间结构中重构扩散函数；通过固定点分析确定物理允许的解，并将局部行为扩展至全局宇宙学演化，发展基于扩散斜率动态演化的重构形式体系。

Result: 确定了物理上可接受的固定点分类，发现了新型物质-扩散缩放解（与幂律扩散扇区对应）以及纯扩散主导的构型，后者能在不借助显式宇宙学常数的情况下驱动晚期加速膨胀。

Conclusion: 建立了直接从相空间动力学构造和分类单模引力下可行扩散宇宙学的系统框架，为扩散斜率在宇宙历史中不同区域间的动态演化和插值提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We investigate cosmological diffusion models in unimodular gravity within a dynamical systems reconstruction framework. By treating the logarithmic slope of the diffusion sector as an invertible dynamical variable, the diffusion function can be systematically reconstructed from the phase-space structure of the cosmological evolution. Under these conditions, we determine the physically admissible fixed points of the system, identifying novel matter--diffusion scaling solutions associated with power-law diffusion sectors, as well as purely diffusion-dominated configurations capable of driving late-time accelerated expansion without requiring an explicit cosmological constant term. The local behavior around the fixed points is then extended to the full cosmological evolution, providing a framework to explore the global implications of diffusion cosmologies. Beyond the asymptotic fixed-point structure, we further develop a reconstruction formalism based on the dynamical evolution of the diffusion slope, allowing for trajectories interpolating between different diffusion regimes during the cosmic history. Our results establish a systematic framework for constructing and classifying viable diffusion cosmologies in unimodular gravity directly from the phase-space dynamics.

</details>


### [164] [Ghost-free higher-gradient Newtonian gravity from the Second Law of Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00317)
*M. Pszota,P. Ván*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种避免Ostrogradsky不稳定性的高阶引力修正新框架。作者证明，将弱非局部状态空间的热力学第二定律应用于自引力流体，可以得到与高阶梯度牛顿引力相同的场方程，但该路径下不稳定性被构造性地消除（势场满足一阶弛豫方程，为梯度流）。该推导还产生了压力张量、能量流、牛顿力定律恒等式以及耗散自由的体黏滞压力交叉耦合。正则性约束给出振幅求和规则Σαi=-1，从而用实验室平方反比定律检验将最大内禀长度限制在4×10^-5 m以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿引力的高阶梯度修正虽然能消除质点奇异性，但其拉格朗日动力学受Ostrogradsky不稳定性的困扰，使得这类理论在理论上不可行。作者试图寻找一条无需拉格朗日变分原理的替代路径来获得相同的场方程，从而本质性地规避这一经典不稳定性。

Method: 作者放弃拉格朗日变分方法，转而将热力学第二定律应用于具有弱非局部状态空间的自引力流体。核心方法要点：(1) 势函数满足一阶弛豫方程，作为以熵密度为Lyapunov函数的梯度流；(2) 当熵为凹函数时，弛豫谱在每个波数上负定；(3) 由推导自然得到压力张量、能量流以及无需额外输入即可复现牛顿力定律的精确恒等式；(4) 利用正则性条件推导Yukawa型修正项的振幅求和规则Σαi=-1。

Result: 该热力学路径得出的场方程与高阶梯度牛顿引力一致，但无Ostrogradsky不稳定性。此外，推导揭示了变分原理无法产生的耗散自由体黏滞压力交叉耦合。振幅求和规则使实验室对平方反比定律的测试将最大内禀长度限制在4×10^-5 m以下，并识别出复根区域是一个经典上一致的Lee-Wick类扇区。

Conclusion: 高阶梯度牛顿引力可以通过热力学第二定律而非拉格朗日变分原理得到，从而结构性消除Ostrogradsky不稳定性。该框架提供了新奇的物理结果（如交叉耦合和修正的力定律）并给出可检验的实验室约束，表明热力学方法是在经典框架下处理非局部引力修正的可行途径。

Abstract: Higher-gradient modifications of Newtonian gravity remove the point-mass singularity, but their Lagrangian dynamics is haunted by the Ostrogradsky instability. We show that the same field equations follow from the Second Law of thermodynamics applied to a self-gravitating fluid with a weakly nonlocal state space, and that on this route the instability is absent by construction: the potential obeys a first-order relaxation equation that is a gradient flow, whose Lyapunov function is the entropy density, and whose relaxation spectrum is negative definite at every wavenumber precisely when the entropy is concave. The derivation also yields the pressure tensor and the energy current, an exact identity that reproduces Newton's force law without further input, and a dissipation-free cross-coupling to the bulk viscous pressure that no variational principle can produce. Regularity constrains the Yukawa-type corrections by an amplitude sum rule, $\sum_iα_i=-1$, so that laboratory tests of the inverse-square law bound the largest internal length below $4\times10^{-5}$~m, and identify the complex-root regime as a classically consistent Lee--Wick-like sector.

</details>


### [165] [A Fast and Scalable Transformer Pipeline for Binary Black Hole Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00339)
*Chayan Chatterjee,Abigail Petulante,Haowei Fu,Yang Hu,Roy Lau,Karan Jani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CASTOR，一个基于Transformer的引力波探测流水线，用于从Advanced LIGO探测器检测双黑洞合并信号。其核心优势在于无需重复评估神经网络即可通过时间滑动廉价估算误警率。在MLGWSC-1基准数据集和约五个月真实O3b观测数据上的评估表明，CASTOR在灵敏度上排名前列，并能恢复GWTC-3目录中大部分训练范围内的可靠事件。相比基于音频基础模型域适应的GW-Whisper架构，CASTOR在灵敏度上显著更优，并将背景估计的计算成本降低了20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着未来十年致密双星合并探测率的预期增长，亟需开发快速、鲁棒且可扩展的匹配滤波替代方案用于引力波搜索。虽然Transformer模型在自然语言和音频处理领域引发了革命，但其在引力波天文学中的应用仍基本未被探索，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 提出CASTOR，一种基于Transformer的符合搜索流水线，用于从Advanced LIGO探测器检测双黑洞引力波信号。模型的关键设计之一是允许通过时间滑动廉价地估算误警率，而无需对神经网络进行重复评估。此外还与GW-Whisper（OpenAI音频基础模型的领域适配）进行了对比基准测试。

Result: 在MLGWSC-1基准数据集上，CASTOR在灵敏度方面跻身最先进的机器学习流水线之列，并能成功恢复GWTC-3目录中训练范围内的大部分可靠事件。在与GW-Whisper的对比中，CASTOR在灵敏度上大幅胜过被改用的音频模型，并将背景估计的计算成本降低了20倍。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，CASTOR为未来观测运行提供了一种高度实用且可扩展的深度学习引力波搜索及经验背景估计方案。

Abstract: With the projected increase in the detection rate of compact-binary coalescences in the coming decade, there is critical need to develop fast, robust, and scalable alternatives to matched filtering for gravitational-wave searches. Transformer models have revolutionized natural language and audio processing but their application to gravitational-wave astronomy is still largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce \castor, a transformer-based coincident search pipeline for detecting binary black hole gravitational-wave signals from Advanced LIGO detectors. One of the major features of our model is that it allows the false-alarm rate to be estimated via time slides cheaply without requiring repeated evaluations of the neural network. We evaluate \castor\ on datasets from the Machine-Learning Gravitational-Wave Search Challenge (MLGWSC-1) and on approximately five months of real O3b observing strain. When tested on benchmark datasets, \castor\ ranks among the most sensitive machine-learning pipelines and successfully recovers the majority of confident events from the GWTC-3 catalog that lie within its training range. We also benchmark \castor\ against another transformer architecture, GW-Whisper, a domain-adaptation of OpenAI's audio foundation model. We find that \castor\ substantially outperforms the repurposed audio model in sensitivity and also reduces the computational cost of background estimation by a factor of 20. Our results demonstrate a highly practical, scalable approach for deep-learning gravitational wave searches and empirical background estimation for future observing runs.

</details>


### [166] [Positive energy from timelike singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00423)
*Fernando Ruiz Ruiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了具有时间平移对称性和类时奇点的渐近AdS与渐近平坦爱因斯坦方程解（包括Kasner类时奇点和负质量黑洞）的引力能量。采用Hawking-Horowitz提出的物理经典作用量的壳上哈密顿量定义（与Abbott-Deser和ADM表达式一致），为处理奇点引入类时调控截断边界，在有限调控参数下计算后令其趋零。所有情形下奇点的能量贡献有限且为正，总引力能量为正；仅无奇点的AdS孤立子具有相对于AdS空间的负引力能量，负质量AdS和Schwarzschild黑洞的能量分别等于AdS和Minkowski空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典引力能量定义（ADM、Abbott-Deser等）通常适用于正则或渐近平坦/AdS时空，但对含有类时奇点的时空（如Kasner奇点、负质量黑洞）如何定义和计算引力能量一直缺乏系统研究。本文旨在拓展Hawking-Horowitz基于物理经典作用量壳上哈密顿量的引力能量定义，使其能处理类时奇点情形，从而检验这类时空的总引力能量是否为正，并探讨奇异时空与AdS孤立子等正能定理的相关问题。

Method: 采用Hawking和Horowitz三十年前提出的物理经典作用量壳上哈密顿量作为引力能量定义（与Abbott-Deser和ADM表达式一致），适用于具时间平移不变性和类时奇点的时空。为绕过奇点引起的作用量发散与叶状结构问题，引入围绕奇点的类时调控截断边界，使其与常时切片上的良定义叶状结构相容；计算在调控参数的有限值下进行，最后取该参数趋零得到能量最终表达式。该方法被应用于多种解，包括Kasner类时奇点时空、负质量AdS和Schwarzschild黑洞等。

Result: 在文中考虑的所有实例中，奇点的能量贡献都是有限且正的，其值依赖于奇点周围的几何，且总引力能量为正。在所综述的解中，仅无奇点的AdS孤立子具有相对于AdS空间的负引力能量。负质量的AdS黑洞与AdS空间具有相同的能量，负质量的Schwarzschild黑洞与Minkowski空间具有相同的能量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过引入类时调控截断边界，Hawking-Horowitz的壳上哈密顿量定义可一致地推广到含类时奇点的时空，并保持引力能量有限且为正。这意味着类时奇点本身对引力能作出正贡献，不破坏正能性质。负质量黑洞与对应的真空参考空间（AdS或Minkowski）具有相同能量，而AdS孤立子作为唯一例外具有负引力能量。该工作为奇异时空中的引力能量定义提供了新的技术框架，并对正能定理在奇异情形下的适用性给出了正面证据。

Abstract: The energy of various asymptotically AdS and asymptotically flat solutions to the Einstein equations with time-translational invariance and a timelike singularity is computed, spaces with Kasner timelike singularities and black holes with negative masses among them. The definition used for gravitational energy is the on-shell Hamiltonian of the physical classical action proposed by Hawking and Horowitz thirty years ago and which agrees with the Abbott-Deser and ADM expressions. To deal with the singularity, a timelike regulating cut-off boundary surrounding the singularity compatible with a well-defined foliation in constant-time slices is introduced, so calculations are performed at a finite value of the regulating parameter, which is set to zero in the final expression for the energy. In all instances considered, the energy contribution from the singularity is finite and positive, its value depends on the geometry around the singularity and is such that the total gravitational energy is positive. Of the solutions reviewed, only the AdS soliton (which has no singularity) has negative gravitational energy relative to AdS space. The AdS and the Schwarzschild black holes with negative masses have respectively the same energies as AdS and Minkowski spaces.

</details>


### [167] [Dynamical evolution of quantum mutual information in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00485)
*Guosen Ma,Fang Rao,Jingjing Hua,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了施瓦西黑洞背景下三模高斯态基于Rényi-2熵的量子互信息的动力学演化，发现物理上不可达的互信息随霍金温度增加并饱和，而物理上可达的互信息表现出非单调温度依赖（先升后降趋于有限渐近值），并建立了子系统间互信息分布的若干约束关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子互信息是刻画量子系统间关联的基本量，但在弯曲时空（特别是施瓦西黑洞背景）下，连续变量系统中基于Rényi-2熵的量子互信息动力学演化尚未得到充分理解，需要探究其与高斯纠缠和量子导引不同的行为特性。

Method: 在施瓦西黑洞背景下构建三模高斯态模型，利用Rényi-2熵定义量子互信息，分析物理上可达与不可达子系统间互信息随霍金温度的动力学演化，并推导子系统间互信息的分布约束关系。

Result: 物理上不可达的互信息随霍金温度单调增加并最终饱和；物理上可达的互信息随温度呈非单调变化（先升高、达峰、后下降趋于有限渐近值），这与高斯纠缠和量子导引在弯曲时空中通常表现出的单调退化行为不同。此外还建立了多项互信息分配的约束关系。

Conclusion: 基于Rényi-2熵的量子互信息在施瓦西黑洞背景下呈现与纠缠和导引不同的新奇非单调演化特性，相关约束关系有助于深化对弯曲时空中连续变量量子关联的理解。

Abstract: Quantum mutual information is a fundamental quantity for characterizing correlations among quantum systems. In this work, we investigate the dynamic evolution of the quantum mutual information based on Rényi-2 entropy for three-mode Gaussian states in the background of a Schwarzschild black hole. We find that the physically inaccessible mutual information increases with Hawking temperature and eventually saturates, while the physically accessible mutual information exhibits nonmonotonic temperature dependence: it first rises, reaches a peak, then declines toward a finite asymptotic value. This nonmonotonic behavior differs from the monotonic degradation typically observed for Gaussian entanglement and steering in curved spacetime. Furthermore, we establish several constraint relations governing the distribution of mutual information among subsystems. These findings contribute to understanding the quantumness of quantum correlations for continuous variable in Schwarzschild spacetime.

</details>


### [168] [Performance of quantum imaginarity in black hole spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00573)
*Shiyan Li,Yimei Yuan,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild时空中三体量子混合态的量子虚数性（imaginarity）动力学演化行为，发现霍金辐射会退化量子虚数性资源，且虚数性在辐射早期出现剧烈变化后缓慢趋近稳定值，并推导了描述虚数性在Rindler视界间非局域再分配的trade-off关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子虚数性（imaginarity）是量子信息处理中的基本量子资源，但其在黑洞时空这一极端引力环境下的动力学行为尚未被充分理解。理解量子资源在弯曲时空中的演化对于深入认识量子信息与引力相互作用具有重要意义。

Method: 在Schwarzschild时空中对三体量子混合态的量子虚数性动力学进行解析和数值研究，分析霍金辐射对虚数性资源的影响，并推导虚数性跨Rindler视界的trade-off关系。

Result: 发现霍金辐射会退化量子虚数性资源，这一行为与纠缠和相干性在霍金效应下的退化规律一致。虚数性在霍金辐射早期阶段出现急剧变化，随后变化缓慢并渐近趋近稳态值。此外，推导出了刻画虚数性在Rindler视界间非局域再分配的多项trade-off关系。

Conclusion: 该研究深化了人们对黑洞时空中量子资源行为的理解，表明量子虚数性在弯曲时空中的演化呈现出与其他量子资源（如纠缠、相干性）相似但具有独特早期瞬变特征的退化模式，并揭示了虚数性在视界间的资源再分配规律。

Abstract: Quantum imaginarity is a fundamental quantum resource essential for quantum information processing. In this paper, we investigate the dynamical evolution behavior of quantum imaginarity for tripartite quantum mixed states in Schwarzschild spacetime. We find that Hawking radiation degrades the quantum resource of imaginarity, consistent with the behavior of entanglement and coherence under the Hawking effect. Interestingly, imaginarity exhibits a sharp change in the early stage of Hawking radiation, after which it varies slowly and asymptotically approaches a steady value. We further derive several trade-off relations that characterize the nonlocal redistribution of imaginarity across Rindler horizons. The findings of this study contribute to a deeper understanding of quantum resource behaviors in black hole spacetimes.

</details>


### [169] [Directional Response Optimization through Linear Recombination of Time-Delay Interferometry Channels in Space-based Gravitational Wave Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00589)
*Heng-Sen Jiao,Jing-Rui Zhang,Hong-Bo Jin,Yun-Long Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对空间引力波探测器中不同时间延迟干涉（TDI）通道对天空不同区域响应各异的问题，提出了一种频率域响应矩阵方法，通过广义特征值问题求解最优权重线性组合，以最大化目标天空区域与抑制天空区域之间的响应比，从而实现对特定天空区域（如银河系双白矮星）的近单色引力波源进行窄带定向搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA、Taiji和TianQin等空间引力波探测器使用TDI来消除不等臂星座中的激光频率噪声。不同TDI观测量（如A、E、T通道，Sagnac组合α、β、γ，以及16链路TDI）对天空的响应各不相同，因此需要一种方法对不同通道进行线性组合，实现对特定天空区域增强响应、对另一区域抑制响应的目的，以提升对近单色源（如银河系双白矮星）的探测灵敏度。

Method: 构建TDI组合的频率域响应矩阵，分别在目标天空区域和待抑制天空区域上做平均；将该问题转化为广义特征值问题，求解使得两个区域响应之比最大化的最优权重向量。在毫赫兹频段对A、E、T通道、Sagnac组合α、β、γ以及16链路TDI等候选通道集进行了验证。

Result: 在选定频率附近可以实现天空区域零点（sky-region null）和较大的区域对比度，特征值ρ从小基组的O(10)到大通道集的O(10²)。目标-抑制比（TSR）在设计频率附近达到峰值并迅速随频率偏离而衰减，表明优化权重向量本质上是窄带的。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于毫赫兹频段近单色引力波源的定向搜索，如已分辨的银河系双白矮星双星系统。优化权重向量具有窄带特性，适合围绕目标频率和天空方向进行针对性搜索。

Abstract: Space-based gravitational-wave detectors such as LISA, Taiji, and TianQin employ time-delay interferometry (TDI) to cancel laser-frequency noise for unequal-arm constellations. Since different TDI observables exhibit distinct sky responses, a linear combination of candidate channels can enhance the average response over one sky region while suppressing that of another. We construct a frequency-domain response matrix for TDI combinations, average it across target and suppressed sky regions, and derive the optimal weights via a generalized eigenvalue problem that maximizes the ratio between these two regional responses. At millihertz frequencies, examples with the $A$, $E$, and $T$ channels, the Sagnac combinations $α$, $β$, and $γ$, and 16-links TDI show that a sky-region null and a large regional contrast are possible near the chosen frequency, with eigenvalues $ρ$ ranging from $\mathcal{O}(10)$ for small bases to $\mathcal{O}(10^2)$ for the larger set. The method is therefore expected to be well suited to nearly monochromatic sources such as the resolved Galactic double white dwarf binaries in the millihertz band. The Target-to-Suppression Ratio (TSR) peaks near the design frequency and falls quickly away from it, so the optimized weight vector is inherently narrowband and suited to targeted searches around a chosen frequency and sky direction.

</details>


### [170] [Circulation and operational sparsity of analogue Hawking radiation in rotating acoustic horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00642)
*Fernando M. Belchior,Joao A. A. S. Reis,Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维旋转排水浴缸声学黑洞中的时间稀疏性（temporal sparsity），分析了旋流对该系统中霍金辐射量子发射时间间隔的影响。研究发现，旋转增强了粒子数流但使频谱变硬，时间重叠程度本质上依赖于用于分辨发射量子的操作时钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间稀疏性是比较发射量子间平均间隔与单量子特征时间尺度的操作量，有助于理解旋转对声学黑洞霍金辐射动力学的影响。此前对旋转排水浴缸声学黑洞的研究主要集中在温度、捕获宽度和透射谱上，而时间结构（即量子发射的时间间隔）尚未被系统研究，特别是旋转如何影响量子发射的时间相关性仍不清晰。

Method: 在二维旋转排水浴缸声学时空中，固定霍金温度由径向排水速率决定（与旋流无关），通过求解径向散射问题计算收敛的灰体因子修正后的粒子数和能量通量（包括超辐射扇区）。比较了两种稀疏性定义：基于热时钟的稀疏性和基于频谱的稀疏性，并检验了分箱稀疏性定义在二维中的红外敏感性。

Result: 在旋流-排水比率为1时，热时钟稀疏性为0.44，而基于频谱的稀疏性为1.82。前者在比率约0.40处穿越时间重叠基准，后者在所有采样旋流比率下均保持大于1。此外，分箱稀疏性定义在二维空间中对红外具有对数敏感性。旋转增强粒子数流的同时使频谱变硬。

Conclusion: 旋转对声学黑洞的量子发射时间结构有显著影响：它增加通量但缩短量子间的平均时间间隔并硬化频谱，导致时间重叠是操作依赖的——不同的操作时钟会给出不同的稀疏性判断。这强调了在定义和测量声学黑洞发射的时间特性时需要谨慎选择适当的操作量。

Abstract: Temporal sparsity compares the mean interval between emitted quanta with a characteristic single-quantum timescale. We study this operational quantity for the rotating draining-bathtub acoustic horizon in two spatial dimensions. The Hawking temperature is fixed by the radial drain and does not depend on circulation, while the geometric capture width and the transmission spectrum do. A geometric black-body benchmark therefore predicts reduced temporal separation as circulation increases. We then solve the radial scattering problem and compute converged greybody-corrected number and energy fluxes, including the superradiant sector. At a circulation-to-drain ratio of unity, the thermal-clock sparsity is 0.44 whereas the spectrum-based sparsity is 1.82. The former crosses the temporal-overlap benchmark near a ratio of 0.40, while the latter remains above unity at all sampled circulation ratios. We also show that the binned sparsity definition is logarithmically infrared sensitive in two spatial dimensions. Rotation therefore enhances the number flux while hardening the spectrum, making temporal overlap intrinsically dependent on the operational clock used to resolve the emitted quanta.

</details>


### [171] [Perturbations and stability of black holes with static scalar hair in general GLPV theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00664)
*Petarpa Boonserm,Antonio De Felice,Ratchaphat Nakarachinda,Shinji Tsujikawa,Pitayuth Wongjun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有径向标量毛的静态球对称黑洞在一般四阶-五阶GLPV理论（含Horndeski）中的背景方程及完整奇偶宇称二次作用量。在正则非退化分支上，该表述跨Killing视界成立，给出局部无鬼条件和径向/角向eikonal特征。奇偶两个宇称扇区的张量模式具有相同的平方径向速度。当径向坐标为类时，反对称混合会诱导两个偶宇称角向分支的非标准大多极标度。要求两个分支具有有限非零的eikonal相速度，会选出四阶和五阶beyond-Horndeski函数之间与Horndeski兼容的条件。在视界上标量动能项有限非零时，精确二次GLPV黑洞的每个非平凡分支局部不稳定或具有简并张量锥。对于标量-Gauss-Bonnet黑洞（视界标量动能消失的情形），作者构造了解析幂律四阶beyond-Horndeski形变，其关联五阶函数由同一条件固定。在足够小的耦合下，外部区域的所有局部无鬼条件和高频梯度稳定性均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义标量-张量引力理论（特别是GLPV/Horndeski框架）中具有标量毛的静态球对称黑洞的稳定性与动力学结构。这些理论超越Horndeski，包含可能导致额外自由度或梯度不稳定的项，因此需要系统化地推导其背景、二次扰动作用量以及无鬼/稳定性条件，以确定哪些黑洞分支在物理上可容许。

Method: 推导静态球对称径向标量毛黑洞在一般四-五阶GLPV理论中的背景场方程和完整奇偶宇称二次作用量。分析方法包括：在正则非退化分支上横跨Killing视界表述；计算不同宇称扇区张量与标量模式的局部无鬼条件及eikonal特征（径向和角向）；分析当径向坐标为类时反对称混合导致的非标准多极标度行为；通过要求eikonal相速度有限非零来推导四阶与五阶beyond-Horndeski函数间的兼容性条件；利用disformal映射将特定分支联系到Horndeski；并构建标量-Gauss-Bonnet黑洞的解析幂律四阶形变并检验其局部稳定性和梯度稳定性。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 在两个宇称扇区中张量模式共享相同的平方径向速度；(2) 类时径向坐标下反对称混合导致两个偶宇称角向分支的非标准大多极标度行为；(3) 要求两个角向分支eikonal相速度有限非零，选出四/五阶beyond-Horndeski函数间的Horndeski兼容条件，该条件对齐余退化方向并允许局部disformal映射到Horndeski；(4) 在视界标量动能有限非零时，精确二次GLPV黑洞的每个非平凡分支局部不稳定或有简并张量锥；(5) 对一般移位反射对称二次GLPV理论与正则Horndeski兼容分支，该阻碍（除少数例外）依然成立；(6) 对标量-Gauss-Bonnet黑洞（视界标量动能为零），构建了解析幂律形变，小耦合下外部无鬼条件与梯度稳定性全部满足；(7) 线性Gauss-Bonnet耦合下估计了内部尺度，低于该尺度背景与稳定性展开失去微扰控制（但不意味着物理不稳定）。

Conclusion: 这项工作系统揭示了GLPV/Horndeski理论中标量毛黑洞的稳定性图景：一般分支在视界处趋于不稳定或退化，只有满足特定兼容条件（即四阶与五阶beyond-Horndeski函数间的Horndeski关系）的正则分支才可能保持良好行为。对于标量-Gauss-Bonnet理论，通过构造合适的beyond-Horndeski形变（关联五阶函数由兼容条件唯一固定），可以在小耦合情形下维持外部区域的稳定性，尽管在线性耦合下存在微扰展开失效的内部尺度，这属于理论截断效应而非物理不稳定。

Abstract: We derive the background equations and complete odd- and even-parity quadratic actions for static, spherically symmetric black holes with radial scalar hair in general quartic-quintic Gleyzes-Langlois-Piazza-Vernizzi (GLPV) theories, including Horndeski. On regular, nondegenerate branches, the formulation applies across Killing horizons and yields local no-ghost conditions and radial and angular eikonal characteristics. Tensor modes in both parity sectors share the same squared radial speed. When the radial coordinate is timelike, generic antisymmetric mixing induces nonstandard large-multipole scaling for both even-parity angular branches. Requiring both branches to have finite, nonzero eikonal phase speeds selects the Horndeski-related compatibility condition between the quartic and quintic beyond-Horndeski functions. This condition aligns the covariant degeneracy directions and permits a local disformal map to Horndeski when regular and invertible. For a finite, nonzero scalar kinetic term at the horizon, every nontrivial branch of the exact quadratic GLPV black hole is locally unstable or has a degenerate tensor cone near a simple outer horizon. Apart from identified exceptions, this obstruction extends to general shift- and reflection-symmetric quadratic GLPV theories and the regular Horndeski-related branch of the quartic-quintic class. Finally, for scalar-Gauss-Bonnet black holes with a vanishing horizon scalar kinetic term, we construct analytic power-law quartic beyond-Horndeski deformations whose associated quintic function is fixed by the same condition. At sufficiently small coupling, all local no-ghost and high-frequency gradient-stability conditions hold throughout the exterior. For a linear Gauss-Bonnet coupling, we estimate an interior scale below which the background and stability expansions lose perturbative control; this does not imply a physical instability.

</details>


### [172] [Reissner-Nordstrom in the Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin electromagnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00882)
*Marco Astorino*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文展示了嵌入Bertotti-Robinson磁场中的磁化Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞，并通过Ernst方程的Harrison变换生成处于Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin磁场中的Reissner-Nordstrom度规，讨论了真空毛状I型Schwarzschild解的广义情形，并通过显式微分同胚证明了Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson各种形式之间的局部等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞在外部磁场背景（如Bertotti-Robinson型均匀磁场）中的行为，探索通过Harrison变换在Ernst方程框架下生成新的精确解，以理解磁场对黑洞时空几何的影响，以及不同形式解之间的深层联系。

Method: 首先构造嵌入Bertotti-Robinson磁场中的磁化Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞作为种子解；随后利用Ernst方程的Harrison变换生成新的Reissner-Nordstrom度规于Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin磁场中；讨论广义的真空毛状I型Schwarzschild特例；最后通过构造显式微分同胚展示Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson不同形式之间的局部等价性。

Result: 成功生成了处于Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin磁场中的Reissner-Nordstrom度量新精确解，涵盖了若干特例（包括真空毛状I型Schwarzschild解的广义形式），并显式构造了微分同胚证明了Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson解的各种表示之间是局部等价的。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了引力场方程在磁场背景下新的精确解族，揭示了Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞在Bertotti-Robinson型磁场中的可解性，并阐明了不同坐标表示下的Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson解本质上描述同一时空几何。

Abstract: The magnetic Reissner-Nordstrom black hole embedded into the Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field is presented. Then it is used, as a seed, to generate, through the Harrison transformation of the Ersnt's equations, the Reissner-Nordstrom metric in the Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin magnetic field. Special cases generalising, the vacuum hairy type I Schwarzschild solution is discussed. The local equivalence between various forms of the Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson is shown by exhibiting the explicit diffeomorphism.

</details>


### [173] [Polarization Images of Neutron Stars Illuminated by a Thin Accretion Disk: A Comparison with Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00934)
*Dan-Dan Feng,Guo-Ping Li,Chen-Yu Yang,Hong Lei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究静态球对称中子星在几何薄吸积盘照射下的线偏振图像，发现总偏振强度与总强度正相关并在星体表面附近达到最大，观测倾角增大会破坏偏振图像对称性，磁场主要影响偏振方向而非强度分布。与Schwarzschild黑洞偏振图像对比，两者在强偏振区域位置和中央无偏振信号区域大小上存在明显差异，表明线偏振图像可提供超出总强度图像的信息用于区分中子星与黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的总强度图像在区分中子星与黑洞等致密天体方面存在局限，需要探索额外观测量来增强对致密天体的识别能力。线偏振图像可能携带超越总强度图像的物理信息，但目前对中子星线偏振图像的系统研究尚不充分。

Method: 采用多方状态方程构建中子星平衡构型；在几何光学近似下求解零测地线方程和线偏振矢量的平行输运方程；通过改变观测者倾角和磁场位型，系统模拟静态度规中子星被薄吸积盘照射时的线偏振图像；引入净电矢量位置角χ_net和第二方位角Fourier模∠β_2来刻画偏振方向的空间结构。

Result: 总偏振强度与总强度呈正相关，在中子星表面附近达到最大值；观测倾角增大导致偏振图像对称性逐步破缺；磁场主要改变偏振矢量方向，对偏振强度分布影响有限；中子星与Schwarzschild黑洞在强偏振区域位置及中央无偏振信号区域大小上呈现显著差异。

Conclusion: 线偏振图像包含总强度图像之外的新颖信息，可作为区分中子星与黑洞的有效观测诊断工具。

Abstract: We investigate linear polarization images of static, spherically symmetric neutron stars illuminated by a geometrically and optically thin accretion disk. Neutron star equilibrium configurations are constructed with a polytropic equation of state, and the null geodesic equations and the parallel transport equation for the linear polarization vector are solved in the geometric optics approximation. The numerical results show that the total polarized intensity is generally positively correlated with the total intensity and reaches its maximum near the neutron star surface. As the observer inclination increases, the symmetry of the polarization images is progressively broken. In addition, the magnetic field configuration mainly affects the direction of the polarization vectors, while its influence on the overall polarized intensity distribution is comparatively limited. To further characterize the spatial structure of the polarization direction, we introduce the net electric vector position angle $χ_{\mathrm{net}}$ and the second azimuthal Fourier mode $\angleβ_2$. A comparison with polarization images of a Schwarzschild black hole reveals clear differences between the two types of compact objects in the locations of strongly polarized regions and the size of the central region without a polarization signal. These results show that linear polarization images provide information beyond total intensity images for distinguishing neutron stars from black holes.

</details>


### [174] [Constraining AGN Disk Properties with Gravitational Waves from Inspiraling Stellar-Mass Binary Black Holes in Hierarchical Triple Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00945)
*Jie Wu,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究使用空间引力波探测器观测嵌入在活动星系核（AGN）盘中的恒星质量双黑洞（BBH）信号，以此约束其局部气体密度。通过演化包含相对论修正和气态动力学摩擦的外轨道，结合Fisher矩阵方法估计统计误差和系统误差，发现GW190521类系统被LISA观测一年可将盘密度约束在σ_ρ~10⁻¹²–10⁻¹⁰ g cm⁻³水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间引力波探测器能在BBH并合前长时间观测其信号，使微弱的环境扰动得以累积。对于位于AGN盘中的BBH，局部气体密度是刻画超大质量黑洞（SMBH）环境和致密天体迁移的关键参数。然而目前对这类环境密度的约束手段有限，作者希望探索能否通过引力波信号来约束该密度。

Method: 作者演化了一个围绕Kerr SMBH的恒星质量BBH外轨道，纳入相对论修正和气态动力学摩擦（DF）；构建包含BBH旋近、de Sitter进动、DF相位修正和运动源效应的探测器坐标系波形；对采样系统进行Fisher矩阵计算，估计参数统计不确定性和系统误差。

Result: 发现较大气体密度通常能提高多个源参数和外轨道参数的统计精度，但当忽略DF时也会增大系统误差。对GW190521类系统由LISA观测一年，盘密度可被约束在σ_ρ~10⁻¹²–10⁻¹⁰ g cm⁻³的水平。

Conclusion: 该约束可将BBH并合环境与星系核气体结构及黑洞增长条件联系起来，表明层次式BBH旋近信号有望探测AGN盘环境，前提是必须一致地建模气体效应。

Abstract: Space-based gravitational-wave detectors can observe stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs) long before merger, allowing weak environmental perturbations to accumulate. For binaries embedded in active galactic nucleus (AGN) disks, the local gas density characterizes the environment of the supermassive black hole (SMBH) and compact-object migration. We study whether such signals can constrain this density when a stellar-mass BBH orbits a Kerr SMBH. We evolve the outer orbit with relativistic corrections and gaseous dynamical friction (DF), and construct the detector-frame waveform including BBH inspiral, de Sitter precession, DF phase correction, and moving-source effects. Using Fisher-matrix calculations for sampled systems, we estimate statistical uncertainties and systematic errors. Larger gas densities generally improve the statistical precision of several source and outer-orbit parameters, but also increase systematic errors when DF is omitted. For favorable GW190521-like systems observed by LISA for one year, the disk density can be constrained at the level of $σ_ρ\sim10^{-12}\text{--}10^{-10}\,{\rm g\,cm^{-3}}$. Such constraints would connect BBH merger environments to the gas structure of galactic nuclei and the conditions that support black hole growth. These results indicate that hierarchical BBH inspirals can probe AGN disk environments, provided that gas effects are modeled consistently.

</details>


### [175] [Near-extremal asymptotics and strong cosmic censorship for black holes immersed in a Chaplygin-like dark fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00980)
*Hao-Peng Yan,Zeng-Yi Zhang,Xiang-Qian Li,Xiao-Jun Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了浸没于Chaplygin-like暗流体(CDF)中的电中性黑洞质量标量微扰的强宇宙监督假设(SCC)。通过解析推导极端与Nariai视界边界参数化，获得近极端黑洞分裂和柯西视界表面引力的显式渐近行为，并结合全局拟正则模计算评估Christodoulou表述的SCC。潜在SCC违背被限制在极端附近的狭窄区域，其宽度随有效宇宙学尺度非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强宇宙监督假设是广义相对论中的重要议题，需要检验其在具有宇宙学常数效应和暗流体背景的黑洞解中的有效性。CDF黑洞具有渐近de Sitter外部解和内部柯西视界，为SCC的检验提供了新场景，但此前缺乏对该类系统的解析控制和定量分析。

Method: 1) 推导极端与Nariai视界边界的闭式参数化；2) 获得近极端视界分裂和柯西视界表面引力的解析渐近表达式；3) 推导标量场拟正则模的近极端主导谱的解析公式；4) 结合全局拟正则模数值计算，确定谱间隙并系统评估Christodoulou表述的SCC。

Result: 近极端基本阻尼率趋近于柯西视界表面引力，而高角量子多重极保留了对CDF极端几何的显式依赖。SCC潜在违背局限于临近极端性的窄区域；随有效宇宙学尺度增大，该区域下边界由de Sitter模、光子球模和de Sitter模依次主导，归一化宽度呈非单调变化。

Conclusion: 该项工作通过解析与数值相结合的手段，精确刻画了三视界域内CDF黑洞的SCC符合性图景，揭示了近极端谱行为与有效宇宙学尺度之间的复杂关联，增进了对广义相对论中宇宙监督假设有效边界的理解。

Abstract: We investigate strong cosmic censorship (SCC) for massless scalar perturbations of electrically neutral black holes immersed in a Chaplygin-like dark fluid (CDF). The matter distribution produces an asymptotically de Sitter exterior while supporting an inner Cauchy horizon. We derive a closed-form parametrization of the extremal and Nariai horizon boundaries. This analytic control yields explicit near-extremal asymptotics for the horizon splitting and Cauchy-horizon surface gravity, together with an analytic expression for the leading near-extremal scalar quasinormal spectrum. The fundamental near-extremal damping rate approaches the Cauchy-horizon surface gravity, whereas higher angular multipoles retain explicit dependence on the CDF extremal geometry. Combining these analytic results with global quasinormal-mode calculations, we determine the spectral gap and assess the Christodoulou formulation of SCC throughout the three-horizon domain. Potential SCC violation is confined to a narrow region near extremality. As the effective cosmological scale increases, the lower boundary of this region is controlled successively by de Sitter, photon-sphere, and de Sitter modes, while its normalized width varies nonmonotonically.

</details>


### [176] [Dissipative Multi-Field Dynamics from Non-Hermitian Inflationary Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00992)
*S. D. Campos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个微扰框架,用于研究具有非最小引力耦合和非厄米势的复标量场驱动的暴胀。可观测宇宙微波背景辐射时期,动力学退化为有效的保守双场模型,保留α-attractor类预测。暴胀末期轨迹弯曲激活非厄米部分,引发几何再加热,产生可计算的阻尼因子,在引力波高频段(f>10² Hz)产生特征性抑制,可被未来探测器检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米(非酉)物理在暴胀宇宙学中的后果,特别是如何在保持CMB尺度可观测量与现有观测数据一致的同时,产生可区分的高频引力波特征信号,为未来引力波实验提供可检验的预言。

Method: 构建具有非最小引力耦合和非厄米势的复标量暴胀微扰框架;分析CMB时期简化为保守双场模型;通过轨迹弯曲激活非厄米扇区,利用复质量本征值计算非酉演化对曲率谱和随机引力波背景的阻尼因子。

Result: CMB尺度可观测量不变,保持α-attractor预言的ns、r和f_NL与Planck 2018及BICEP/Keck约束一致;在高频引力波谱中出现特征性抑制(f>10² Hz),可能被爱因斯坦望远镜和Big Bang Observer等未来探测器检验。

Conclusion: 非厄米复标量暴胀框架在CMB尺度上保持与标准α-attractor一致的预测,同时通过几何再加热的非酉演化在高频引力波波段引入可计算的独特抑制信号,为该类模型提供了可检验的实验预言。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a perturbative framework for inflation driven by a complex inflaton with non-minimal gravitational coupling and a non-Hermitian potential. During the observable cosmic microwave background radiation era, the dynamics reduce to an effectively conservative two-field model, preserving the predictions of the $α$-attractor class and satisfying Planck 2018 and BICEP/Keck constraints on $n_s$, $r$, and $f_{\mathrm{NL}}$. Near the end of inflation, trajectory bending activates the non-Hermitian sector, triggering geometric reheating. The resulting non-unitary evolution modifies the curvature spectrum and stochastic gravitational-wave background through a calculable damping factor determined by the complex mass eigenvalues. While cosmic microwave background-scale observables remain essentially unchanged, a distinctive suppression emerges in the high-frequency gravitational-wave spectrum ($f > 10^2$ Hz), potentially testable by future detectors such as the Einstein Telescope and the Big Bang Observer.

</details>


### [177] [Charging higher-dimensional spacetimes with a generalized Kerr-Schild transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01012)
*Aravindhan Srinivasan,Marcello Ortaggio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过广义Kerr-Schild变换，以测地零向量场k为种子构造高维Einstein-Maxwell(-Chern-Simons)解，得到了三个不同的解分支，并发现Chern-Simons项在壳上恒为零；此外还扩展了六维带电Taub-NUT度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维引力理论（如弦论、超引力）中Einstein-Maxwell和Chern-Simons解具有重要物理意义，但构造此类精确解的方法有限。作者试图利用广义Kerr-Schild变换这一几何工具，从真空种子解系统地构造高维带电解，并探索解的完整分类。

Method: 采用广义Kerr-Schild变换，沿测地零向量场k进行构造。假设矢量势A与k对齐，且k为满足"光学约束"的Weyl对齐零方向。依据k的膨胀和扭曲性质进行分类讨论：膨胀+扭曲分支、膨胀+无扭曲分支（再按剪切是否为零分为两类）、非膨胀分支。在A不对齐的放宽假设下，额外导出六维带电Taub-NUT度量的扩展。

Result: 1) 膨胀且扭曲时剪切必为零，包含带电Taub-NUT度量；2) 膨胀且无扭曲时得到两类解：Robinson-Trautman电真空解（非零Maxwell场，剪切为零）和剪切解（零场）；3) 非膨胀情形归约到Kundt解。所有情形下Chern-Simons项在壳上恒为零。还获得了六维带电Taub-NUT度量扩展，其磁场强度为两个不同Kähler 2-形式的线性组合。

Conclusion: 广义Kerr-Schild变换为从真空种子构造高维Einstein-Maxwell解提供了一个统一的框架，完整地分类了A与k对齐情形下的所有可能分支。Chern-Simons项在壳上恒为零这一事实表明此类构造不产生非平凡的高维Chern-Simons修正。六维Taub-NUT解的发现拓展了高维带电解的知识，其双Kähler形式的磁结构具有新意。

Abstract: We explore the construction of higher-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell(-Chern-Simons) solutions from vacuum seeds by means of a generalized Kerr-Schild transformation along a geodesic null vector field $\mathbf{k}$. Assuming the vector potential $\mathbf{A}$ to be aligned with $\mathbf{k}$, and $\mathbf{k}$ to be a Weyl aligned null direction satisfying the ``optical constraint'', we arrive at three distinct branches of solutions. If $\mathbf{k}$ is expanding and twisting, then its shear must vanish -- this branch includes certain charged Taub-NUT metrics. If $\mathbf{k}$ is expanding and twistfree, one finds two subfamilies: Robinson-Trautman electrovac solutions with a non-null Maxwell field if the shear is zero, or shearing solutions with a null field. Finally, the case when $\mathbf{k}$ is non-expanding reduces to a subset of the known Kundt solutions. In all cases, the Chern-Simons term turns out to be identically zero on-shell. In passing, by relaxing the alignment assumption on $\mathbf{A}$, we also obtain a six-dimensional extension of a charged Taub-NUT metric for which the magnetic part of the field strength is a linear combination of two distinct Kähler 2-forms, as opposed to the previously known examples in more than four dimensions.

</details>


### [178] [Ultraviolet Closure in Asymptotically Weyl-Invariant Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01047)
*Daniel Coumbe*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Palatini形式下的渐近Weyl不变引力（AWIG），其指数n在爱因斯坦型红外极限（n→1）与Weyl不变紫外极限（n→2）之间插值。在四维中，最小标量Palatini f(R)扇区内精确Weyl不变性唯一地选定R²作用量为紫外端点。对严格紫外理论的分析表明，在正则分支（R≠0）及受限的无挠、偶宇称纯曲率扇区内，任意圈阶的所有允许发散在壳计数器项都归结为原始√−gR²项加上拓扑Euler密度，为其相对于爱因斯坦引力具有更好的紫外行为提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦引力在紫外区不可重整化，需要寻找具有更好紫外行为的引力理论。Weyl不变引力因其特殊的对称性和紫外性质而被视为可能的候选者，但需要理解如何从Weyl不变紫外区域平滑过渡到描述现实世界的爱因斯坦型红外区域。

Method: 作者在Palatini形式中研究AWIG，定义插值指数n（红外n→1，紫外n→2）。首先通过最小标量Palatini f(R)系统中的Weyl不变性约束来确定R²作用量为唯一紫外端点；然后解释指数为有效曲率依赖量，约束一类满足端点条件的平滑自治流；最后在正则分支R≠0且限制在无挠、偶宇称纯曲率扇区内，逐圈阶分析所有允许发散的在壳计数器项。

Result: 精确Weyl不变性在四维最小标量Palatini f(R)扇区中唯一选择R²作用量作为紫外端点。在严格紫外理论中，任意圈阶的允许发散在壳计数器项均简化为√−gR²项加上拓扑Euler密度项。该条件性结果虽未完全建立离壳微扰重整化性质，但相对于爱因斯坦引力提供了改善紫外行为的非平庸证据，并指出了未来第一性原理重整化群计算的具体目标。

Conclusion: AWIG框架为从Weyl不变紫外理论到爱因斯坦型红外行为的插值提供了统一描述，并为理解引力理论在紫外区的可重整化性提供了新线索。作者识别出R²作用量为精确Weyl不变端点，并部分证明了其紫外发散的受限结构，这为未来更完整的重整化群分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: We investigate asymptotically Weyl-invariant gravity (AWIG) in the Palatini formulation, defined by an exponent n that interpolates between an Einstein-like infrared regime, where n goes to one, and a Weyl-invariant ultraviolet regime, where n goes to two. In four dimensions, we show that exact Weyl invariance within the minimal scalar Palatini f(R) sector uniquely selects the R^2 action as the ultraviolet endpoint. Interpreting the exponent as an effective curvature-dependent quantity, we constrain a phenomenological class of smooth autonomous flows compatible with the required infrared and ultraviolet endpoints. We then analyse the strict ultraviolet theory. On the regular branch R not equal to zero, and within a restricted torsionless, parity-even sector constructed purely from curvature, all admissible divergent on-shell counterterms reduce at arbitrary loop order to the original \sqrt{-g}R^2 term plus a topological Euler density. Although this conditional result does not establish full off-shell perturbative renormalizability, it provides nontrivial evidence for improved ultraviolet behaviour relative to Einstein gravity and identifies a concrete target for a future first-principles renormalization-group calculation.

</details>


### [179] [High-Frequency Gravitational-Wave Transduction in a SQUID-Terminated Superconducting Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01054)
*H. Hadil,Amin Rezaei Akbarieh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了以SQUID终结的超导微波腔作为高频引力波（MHz–GHz范围）窄带参量换能器的理论响应框架。作者推导了四分之一波模谱及本征频率对线长、相速度和SQUID感应长度变化的一阶响应，并引入了两个独立的核函数（共振频率响应和光子对产生响应），在Ω_GW≈2ω_n附近将孤立模动力学约化为Mathieu型参量放大器，给出了光子数、正交方差、增益和不稳定阈值的完整描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 兆赫兹到吉赫兹范围的高频引力波超出了传统干涉仪的探测能力，需要发展新的检测策略。理解SQUID终结超导微波腔作为窄带参量换能器的工作原理，是探索该频段引力波探测途径的理论基础。

Method: 利用腔边界条件和约瑟夫森电感对磁通的依赖推导四分之一波模谱及本征频率的一阶响应；将微扰动力学投影到静态腔模上，揭示共振频率响应与光子对产生响应的一般性区别；在Ω_GW≈2ω_n附近将孤立模动力学简化为含耗散的Mathieu型参量放大器，分析自发光子产生与相位敏感响应。

Result: 引入了两个独立的核函数R_n^ω和R_n^pair，它们仅在模归一化和空间轮廓变化可忽略时一致。自发光子产生随引力波应变二次方增长且对代表性参数极其微弱，而带相干探针的相位敏感响应可随应变线性增长。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了窄带引力波换能的理论描述，但要获得定量灵敏度估计，仍需器件特定的机械-电磁响应标定、模隔离验证以及对损耗和噪声的完整处理。

Abstract: High-frequency gravitational waves in the MHz--GHz range require detection strategies beyond conventional interferometers. We study the response of a SQUID-terminated superconducting microwave cavity as a narrowband parametric transducer. The cavity boundary conditions and the flux dependence of the Josephson inductance are used to derive the quarter-wave mode spectrum and the first-order eigenfrequency response to changes in the physical line length, phase velocity, and SQUID inductive length. By projecting the perturbed dynamics onto the static cavity modes, we show that the resonance-frequency response and the photon-pair-production response are generally distinct. We therefore introduce two independent kernels, $R_n^ω$ and $R_n^{\rm pair}$, which coincide only when changes in mode normalization and spatial profiles can be neglected. Near $Ω_{\rm GW}\simeq 2ω_n$, the isolated-mode dynamics reduce to a Mathieu-type parametric amplifier, allowing the photon number, quadrature variances, gain, and instability threshold to be obtained in the presence of dissipation. Spontaneous photon production scales quadratically with the gravitational-wave strain and is extremely small for representative parameters, whereas phase-sensitive responses with a coherent probe can scale linearly with strain. The framework therefore provides a theoretical description of narrowband gravitational-wave transduction, while a quantitative sensitivity estimate requires device-specific calibration of the mechanical--electromagnetic response, verification of mode isolation, and a complete treatment of loss and noise.

</details>


### [180] [Colored-Noise-Induced Horizon Fluctuations Near a Double Root in an Effective Black-Hole Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01063)
*Kashif Ammar Yasir*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个准静态球对称随机半经典模型，基于量子Langevin方程的响应-噪声结构，研究应力张量涨落如何通过几何响应（如内-外视界根合并时的逆斜率行为）放大视界涨落、温度-视界协方差及灰体滤波光度，并提供从有色度规噪声到相关几何、热与辐射统计量的单实现映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应力张量涨落携带平均值半经典爱因斯坦方程所缺失的信息，但其可观测效应不仅取决于噪声本身，更取决于几何的响应。现有研究缺乏一个能够系统分离源协方差（噪声）与几何响应（尤其是近临界放大）的可操作框架。

Method: 论文提出一个有效的准静态球对称随机半经典模型，由量子Langevin方程的响应-噪声结构组织。模型将预设的平均dressing函数与视界局域化的有色源视为独立变量；对每个源实现重构lapse函数，定位外捕获视界并计算该根处的斜率的绝热Hayward-Kodama温度。通过分析平均lapse趋近内外根合并时的逆斜率行为，将其视为几何磁化率。

Result: 当平均lapse接近内外根合并时，固定源协方差会产生增强的视界涨落、视界-温度协方差，以及正偏斜的灰体滤波光度。该模型实现了从有色度规噪声到关联的几何、热和辐射统计量的单实现映射，并明确区分了储层强度与近临界放大的作用。

Conclusion: 该模型作为随机半经典测试平台，而非Unruh态响应与噪声核的微观评估。其贡献在于提供了一个揭示噪声与几何响应耦合机制的可操作框架，有助于理解应力张量涨落如何通过近临界几何放大产生可观测的统计效应。

Abstract: Stress-tensor fluctuations carry information that is absent from the mean
  semiclassical Einstein equation, but their observable effect depends as much
  on the response of the geometry as on the noise itself. We formulate an
  effective, quasistationary spherical model organized by the response--noise
  structure of a quantum Langevin equation. A prescribed mean dressing and a
  horizon-localized colored source are varied independently. For every source
  realization we reconstruct the lapse, locate its outer trapping horizon, and
  evaluate the adiabatic Hayward--Kodama temperature from the slope at that same
  root. As the mean lapse approaches an inner--outer root merger, its inverse
  slope acts as a geometric susceptibility: a fixed source covariance produces
  enhanced horizon fluctuations, horizon--temperature covariance, and a
  positively skewed, greybody-filtered luminosity. The specific advance is a
  single-realization map from colored metric noise to correlated geometric,
  thermal, and radiative statistics, together with an explicit separation of
  reservoir strength from near-critical amplification. The model is a
  stochastic-semiclassical testbed, not a microscopic evaluation of Unruh-state
  response and noise kernels.

</details>


### [181] [Relative-Phase Control of Common-Bridge Survival in Collisions of Radially Excited Boson Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01134)
*Bo-Xuan Ge*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究发现相对相位主要控制径向激发玻色星正面碰撞中公共桥的存活（维持）而非初始形成。相位偏移越大，桥的持续性越短，该抑制效应在多种玻色星构型和标量势下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索径向激发玻色星碰撞中相对相位如何影响碰撞后公共桥结构的演化和稳定性，以理解玻色星非线性动力学中相位这一关键参数的作用。

Method: 改变相对相位参数，对比不同玻色星构型与不同标量势下碰撞后的桥演化行为；通过采样多个相位值系统分析公共桥的形成与持续性差异。

Result: 基准情形下所有采样相位均能形成公共桥，但较大相位偏移会降低桥的持续性；该抑制现象在所研究的全部构型和势函数中均保持一致。

Conclusion: 相对相位是决定公共桥存活时间的关键控制参数，而非其能否形成的决定因素；这一结论具有跨构型和跨势函数的普适性。

Abstract: We show that the relative phase primarily controls the survival, rather than the initial formation, of the common bridge in head-on collisions of radially excited boson stars. We vary the relative phase and track the post-contact bridge evolution across different boson-star configurations and scalar potentials. In the benchmark, all sampled phases form a common bridge, while larger phase offsets reduce its persistence; the same suppression persists across the configurations and potentials studied.

</details>


### [182] [Josephson interferometry in an Oppenheimer--Snyder-like scale-dependent black-hole spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01177)
*Ali Ovgun,Reggie C. Pantig,G. Lambiase*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个协变框架，用于分析Oppenheimer-Snyder类坍缩产生的尺度依赖外部时空中Josephson输运、超导干涉和并联噪声行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索弯曲时空中超导约瑟夫森效应的协变描述，特别是引力坍缩背景下度规因子对约瑟夫森频率、功率、干涉包络及噪声谱的影响，建立引力与凝聚态物理之间的理论桥梁。

Method: 采用协变框架处理约瑟夫森输运与超导干涉，考虑尺度依赖外部几何（类Oppenheimer-Snyder坍缩），推导径向零传播时间核，分析近极值对数增强向外极值行为交叉过度的机制；并导出红移RCSJ方程、直流和微波驱动下的双结干涉包络，以及Hartle-Hawking态下的噪声谱和fluctuation-delay关系。

Result: 静态约瑟夫森频率与输运电流相对Killing时间获得一个lapse因子，功率获得两个；随坍缩面共动的结中坐标时间相位率与无穷远接收频率因零传播和多普勒效应而不同。近极值对数增强在固定发射偏移下交叉到极点控制的极值行为。在可忽略总电感极限下，静态lapse失衡改变瓣幅值但不移动中心，微波诱导平移需要动力学磁通闭合。Hartle-Hawking态中Tolman红移产生超导排除层与lapse无关的渐近并联噪声谱，低频极限满足无参数fluctuation-delay关系。还导出了shadow-Josephson一致性关系及运行参数的灵敏度界限。

Conclusion: 该工作为引力坍缩背景下超导约瑟夫森效应提供了完整的协变理论框架，揭示了度规因子与多普勒效应、零传播延迟等对超导输运和干涉的耦合机制，并提出了可用于检验引力坍缩参数的观测一致性约束。

Abstract: We develop a covariant framework for Josephson transport, superconducting interference, and shunt noise in the scale-dependent exterior generated by an Oppenheimer--Snyder-like collapse. Static Josephson frequencies and transported currents referred to Killing time acquire one lapse factor, whereas power acquires two. For a junction comoving with the collapsing surface, the coordinate-time phase rate differs from the frequency received at infinity because of null propagation and Doppler effects. We derive the exact radial-null travel-time kernel and show that its local near-extremal logarithmic enhancement crosses over, at fixed emission offset, to pole-controlled extremal behavior. We also obtain the redshifted resistively and capacitively shunted-junction equation and the dc and microwave-driven two-junction interference envelopes. In the negligible-total-inductance limit, static lapse imbalance modifies lobe amplitudes without shifting their centers, whereas microwave-induced translations require dynamical fluxoid closure. In a Hartle--Hawking state, Tolman redshift produces a superconducting exclusion layer and a lapse-independent asymptotic shunt-noise spectrum; its low-frequency limit obeys a parameter-free fluctuation--delay relation. Finally, we derive a shadow--Josephson consistency relation and sensitivity bounds on the running parameter.

</details>


### [183] [Deviations from Kerr: Polar critical curves and photon rings in a class of separable spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01290)
*Hao-Peng Yan,Xiang-Qian Li,Xiao-Jun Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为极向观测者建立了超越Kerr时空的临界曲线和光子环的统一解析描述，针对分离零测地线的静态轴对称时空径向类，推导了临界半径、Lyapunov指数、时间延迟和旋转参数的精确表达式及一阶弱偏差公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞临界曲线和光子环是强场几何的互补探针，现有描述多局限于Kerr时空，缺乏对非Kerr时空的统一解析框架。作者旨在建立超越Kerr的统一描述，并与Kerr预言进行直接比较。

Method: 工作针对极向观测者、具有可分零测地线的稳态轴对称时空径向类展开。通过零测地线动力学选出的自然光学组合组织时空依赖性，采用Kerr相对表示以适应弱Kerr偏差。推导了极向临界半径、Lyapunov指数、时间延迟和旋转参数的紧凑精确表达式，并给出一般一阶公式（需提供具体度规的形变函数）。

Result: 弱偏差分析表明，组合极向观测量仅探测临界轨道处有限的一组局部形变值及其径向导数，而非完整的径向轮廓。推导了各可观测量的紧凑精确表达式及通用一阶公式。

Conclusion: 该统一解析描述为超越Kerr时空的极向光子环和临界曲线观测提供了理论框架，并揭示了弱偏差情形下观测量对局部几何而非全局形变的敏感性，具有重要的理论简化意义。

Abstract: Black-hole critical curves and photon rings provide complementary probes of strong-field geometry, motivating a unified analytic description of these observables beyond Kerr and a direct comparison with the Kerr predictions. We develop such a description for polar observers in a radial class of stationary, axisymmetric spacetimes with separable null geodesics. The spacetime dependence is organized through natural optical combinations selected by the null dynamics and a Kerr-relative representation suited to weak deviations from Kerr. We derive compact exact expressions for the polar critical radius, Lyapunov exponent, time delay, and rotation parameter, together with general first-order formulas that can be applied once the deformation functions of a specific metric are supplied. The weak-deviation analysis shows that the combined polar observables probe only a finite set of local deformation values and radial derivatives at the critical orbit, rather than the full radial profiles.

</details>


### [184] [Gravitational-wave signatures of primordial black hole clusters and the imprint of an early dense-core collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01331)
*Marc Barceló-Sastre,Juan García-Bellido*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过N体模拟研究非自旋原初黑洞（PBH）聚团的动力学自旋生成机制，发现产生可观测量级自旋（χrms~0.1-0.2）需要极致密的星团核心（Plummer半径~10^-5 pc），而长期存活需要秒差距尺度结构，两者冲突可通过"致密核心+延展晕"的双分量结构化解，并预言了LISA频段的随机引力波背景及~50 M⊙的质量截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBHs应具有非自旋且强成团的本征特征，任何观测到的自旋必然是动力学产生的。作者希望量化PBH星团在何种初始条件下能够通过动力学捕获并合生成足够大的自旋、质量及随机引力波背景信号，以与当前引力波观测约束相匹配。

Method: 使用改进的nbody6++gpu N体模拟代码，专门处理近抛物线动力学捕获并合。将"致密核心+延展晕"的两个动力学区域作为独立模块分别模拟：核心块（4000个PBH，Plummer半径3×10^-6至10^-4 pc）在数天至数年内塌缩并触发非准圆并合；晕区域则负责长期存活与持续并合。

Result: 在30-50 M⊙质量范围产生均方根自旋χrms~0.1-0.2需要初始Plummer半径~10^-5 pc量级，而宇宙学时间存活需要秒差距尺度，存在尖锐张力。核心块并合轨道呈强非圆（e→1）且无量纲轨道角动量低，导致残骸自旋系统性低于准圆并合的χ~0.7。核心并合产生峰值为10^-6至10^-4 Hz的随机背景（LISA可观测）。自旋和质量数据均支持在~50 M⊙附近截断的原初质量函数。

Conclusion: PBH簇的双分量结构（致密短命核心+延展长命晕）天然解决了自旋生成与存活时间之间的矛盾。该图景将观测可测量进行了明确分工：自旋由原初塌缩（核心）决定，可探测并合由长期晕决定，随机背景呈双分量特征，且自旋与质量信息共同指向~50 M⊙的质量截断。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) are expected to be born essentially non-spinning and, if the primordial curvature fluctuations possess non-Gaussian tails, strongly clustered. Any spin measured in a PBH population must therefore be dynamically generated. Using direct $N$-body simulations with \textsc{nbody6++gpu}, modified to treat nearly parabolic dynamical-capture mergers, we quantify the conditions under which an initially non-spinning PBH cluster develops the spin, mass and stochastic-background signatures that gravitational-wave observations are now sensitive to. We find a sharp tension: producing a root-mean-square spin $χ_{\rm rms}\sim0.1 - 0.2$ in the $30 - 50\,M_\odot$ range requires initial Plummer radii of order $10^{-5}\,{\rm pc}$, whereas survival over cosmological times requires parsec-scale clusters. This tension is resolved naturally if PBHs form with a compact, short-lived core embedded in an extended halo, and we therefore simulate the two regimes as independent blocks. The core block ($4000$ PBHs, Plummer radii $3\times10^{-6} - 10^{-4}\,{\rm pc}$) collapses in days to years, producing a burst of mergers whose orbits are strongly non-circular ($e\to1$) and of low dimensionless orbital angular momentum, so that remnant spins are systematically below the quasi-circular value $χ\simeq0.7$. Redshifted from their formation epoch, the core mergers deposit a stochastic background peaking in the $10^{-6}$--$10^{-4}\,{\rm Hz}$ band accessible to LISA. The picture predicts a specific division of labour between observables, spins set in the primordial collapse, detectable mergers set by the long-term halo, and a two-component stochastic background, and, in particular, that both the spin and the mass data favour a primordial mass function truncated near $50\,M_\odot$.

</details>


### [185] [Scalar wave scattering by black holes embedded in dark matter halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01391)
*Shi-Qian Hu,Federico Piazza*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了嵌入暗物质晕（Hernquist分布）中的静态黑洞对无质量标量波的散射问题，综合运用零测地线、光辉近似、分波展开和复合角动量（CAM）方法进行分析，发现暗物质晕对散射干涉图样的主要影响可以通过等效的真空施瓦西黑洞频移来近似，但存在不可吸收的残余晕贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的黑洞并非存在于真空中，而是嵌入在由暗物质构成的星系晕内。研究暗物质环境对黑洞引力散射观测特征的影响，有助于更准确地从引力波和电磁波观测中推断黑洞参数，并理解环境效应对黑洞"阴影"和干涉图样的修正。

Method: 采用Hernquist密度分布建模暗物质晕（该分布下爱因斯坦方程存在精确解）；分别通过四条独立途径分析散射：零测地线分析、光辉近似、分波展开/部分波展开以及复合角动量(CAM)方法/Regge极点分析；通过系统对比环境黑洞与真空施瓦西黑洞的散射特征来分离环境效应。

Result: （1）暗物质晕对光环半径影响较弱，但显著移动临界冲击参数和光辉冲击参数；（2）黑洞特征干涉图样得以保留，晕的致密度是主要修正因素，尺度半径起次要作用；（3）足够致密的晕会诱导可观的重力红移；（4）干涉极值的大部分位移可由具有适当ωM_BH的真空施瓦西解复现，但存在无法被等效频移吸收的真实晕贡献；（5）CAM重建表明仅需少量Regge极点即可在较大散射角达到高精度。

Conclusion: 暗物质环境对黑洞标量波散射的干涉图样主要起"等效频移"作用，大部分环境效应可被有效参数吸收，但暗物质晕本身仍留下不可忽略的物理指纹；这对从观测中区分黑洞本征性质与环境效应的策略具有重要启示。

Abstract: We study the scattering of massless scalar waves by static black holes embedded in dark matter halos. The halo is modelled by means of a Hernquist profile, for which the Einstein equations admit an exact solution. We analyse the scattering using null geodesics, the glory approximation, the partial wave expansion, and the complex angular momentum (CAM) method.
  The geometric analysis shows that the presence of the halo changes the light ring radius weakly but shifts the critical and glory impact parameters. The wave analysis shows that the characteristic black hole interference pattern is preserved, with halo compactness providing the leading correction and the halo scale radius playing a secondary role. For sufficiently compact halos, the environment also induces a sizeable gravitational redshift.
  Most of the displacement of the interference extrema can be reproduced by a vacuum Schwarzschild solution with an appropriately chosen $ωM_{\rm BH}$. A non-vanishing residual, however, reveals a genuine halo contribution that cannot be absorbed into such an effective frequency shift. Finally, we perform the CAM reconstruction of the scattering cross section and find that only a few Regge poles are required to achieve high accuracy at large angles.

</details>


### [186] [Bounded Electromagnetic Power Flux at Naked Kasner Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01536)
*Franco Fiorini,P. A. González,Yerko Vásquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了经典电磁场与强曲率裸奇点"共存"的可能性：在Kasner时空的裸奇点附近，通过Plebanski-Tamm映射将弯曲时空中的Maxwell理论转化为平坦空间中有效各向异性介质问题，发现存在规则的静电场构型和电磁波，其功率通量在接近奇点时保持有界，甚至出现零或非零的有限功率通量，使奇点表现为反射镜或辐射吸收/产生的源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究经典电磁场能否在强曲率裸奇点附近保持有界/规则，特别是在Kasner时空这类具有裸奇点的时空中，电磁功率通量能否在奇点的任意邻域内有界，从而探讨奇点与辐射场相互作用的物理特性。

Method: 利用Plebanski-Tamm映射，将弯曲时空中的Maxwell理论等价地转化为平坦空间中有效各向异性介质中的描述，然后在该框架下分析Kasner时空裸奇点附近的静电场和电磁波构型及其功率通量行为。

Result: 在Kasner时空裸奇点附近找到了规则的静电场构型以及电磁波解，其幅度对应接近奇点时有明确定义的、有界的功率通量。在某些情形下奇点表现为完美反射镜；在另一些情形下则存在非零的有限功率通量，可解释奇点吸收或产生辐射场的机制。

Conclusion: 经典电磁场可以在某些裸奇点附近与强曲率"共存"，功率通量保持有界；奇点可能表现为反射镜或辐射的吸收/产生源，这为理解奇点与辐射场的相互作用提供了新的物理图景。

Abstract: In some occasions, it would appear that classical electromagnetic fields could be able to coexist with strong curvature, naked singularities, in the sense that the electromagnetic power flux would be bounded in any open neighborhood of them. We illustrate this rare behavior by considering the naked singularity present in Kasner spacetimes. There, using the Plebanski--Tamm map between Maxwell theory in curved spacetime and the associated description corresponding to an effective anisotropic medium in flat space, we found not only some regular electrostatic field configurations, but also electromagnetic waves whose amplitudes lead to a well defined, bounded power flux as they approach the singularity. Sometimes, in these unusual situations, the singularity resembles a mirror, perfectly reflecting its environs; in other occasions, the existence of a non-null, finite power flux as the waves travel to or from the singular plane, offers a very simple explanation as to how the singularity might absorb or create radiation fields.

</details>


### [187] [Enabling gravitational-wave astronomy with spin-precessing black holes on generic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01568)
*Aldo Gamboa,Lorenzo Pompili,Alessandra Buonanno,Luca Sebastiani,Raffi Enficiaud,Michael Boyle,Lawrence E. Kidder,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Antoni Ramos-Buades,Mark A. Scheel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍SEOBNRv6EPHM——有效单体（EOB）框架中首个支持任意轨道（偏心轨道+进动自旋）的通用引力波模型。该模型覆盖旋进-合并-铃宕全过程，并扩展到动力学捕获和散射事件。通过对1437个准圆和87个偏心自旋进动BBH数值相对论模拟的系统测试，中位波形失配低于1%，与准圆模型SEOBNRv5PHM精度相当，比现有最优通用轨道模型TEOBResumS-Dalí快4倍。模型在准圆极限下比SEOBNRv5PHM快2-3倍，比TEOBResumS-Dalí快一个数量级。作为概念验证，对11个引力波事件进行分析，强化了GW200129的偏心率证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密的恒星环境或层级三星系统中形成的双黑洞（BBH）可能以偏心轨道并合，并携带任意方向的自旋，这些特征会在引力波信号中留下独特印记。现有的EOB族模型虽然支撑了LIGO-Virgo分析，但缺乏同时处理偏心轨道和自旋进动的通用模型。为准确解译探测器中日益丰富的引力波事件，需要能够联合描述这两种物理效应的快速、精确波形模型。

Method: 作者在SEOBNR有效单体框架内构建了首个通用轨道（generic-orbit）自旋进动模型SEOBNRv6EPHM。该模型涵盖双黑洞吸积-合并-铃宕全过程，并扩展至动力学捕获和散射情形。作者使用1437个准圆和87个偏心自旋进动BBH的数值相对论波形进行了系统精度评估，并对比了GW事件分析。

Result: 中位波形失配低于1%，与准圆模型SEOBNRv5PHM精度相当，且比TEOBResumS-Dalí改进约4倍；在准圆极限下比SEOBNRv5PHM快2-3倍，比TEOBResumS-Dalí快约一个数量级。模型能再现NR模拟中通用自旋BBH散射的非微扰现象。对11个引力波事件的分析强化了GW200129的偏心率证据，并通过注入-恢复研究得到支持。

Conclusion: SEOBNRv6EPHM首次实现了在引力波分析中同时准确且高效地考虑偏心率与自旋进动效应，将偏心推断的计算成本降低到当前准圆分析的量级，为下一代引力波天文学提供了关键工具。

Abstract: Binary black holes (BBHs) formed in dense stellar environments or in hierarchical triples can coalesce on eccentric orbits and carry spins of arbitrary orientation, leaving distinctive imprints on their gravitational-wave (GW) emission. We present SEOBNRv6EPHM: the first generic-orbit, spin-precessing model in the effective-one-body SEOBNR family, whose waveforms have underpinned LIGO-Virgo GW analyses since 2011. The model describes the dynamics and multipolar GW signal of generic BBHs, covering the inspiral-merger-ringdown of coalescing binaries and extending to dynamical captures and scattering encounters. We perform the first systematic accuracy assessment of a generic-orbit model against numerical relativity (NR) waveforms of spin-precessing BBHs, using 1437 quasi-circular (QC) and 87 eccentric simulations: median waveform mismatches remain below $1 \%$, matching the accuracy of the QC model SEOBNRv5PHM, and improving on the state-of-the-art generic-orbit model TEOBResumS-Dalí by a median factor of $ 4 $. The model also reproduces the non-perturbative phenomenology observed in NR simulations of generic-spin BBH scattering. It is $\sim 2 - 3$ times faster than SEOBNRv5PHM in the QC limit, and up to an order of magnitude faster than TEOBResumS-Dalí, bringing eccentric inference to the cost of current QC analyses. As a proof of principle, we analyze eleven GW events and focus on GW200129, strengthening its evidence for eccentricity $-$ a result supported by injection-recovery studies with synthetic NR signals of eccentric, spin-precessing BBHs. SEOBNRv6EPHM thus enables, for the first time, accurate and efficient GW analyses that jointly account for eccentricity and spin precession.

</details>

### [188] [Floor-crossing for Legendrian Clasp Move](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01381)
*Filip Strakoš*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文研究了维度至少为2的Legendrian子流形的clasp移动对Chekanov-Eliashberg代数微分中涉及的伪全纯曲线模空间的影响，据此计算了高维twist spheres Λ_k的Chekanov-Eliashberg代数的微分；并构造了无限族Legendrian子流形，其Chekanov-Eliashberg代数允许augmentation，但对足够大的k不存在具有消失Maslov类的精确嵌入Lagrangian填充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维Legendrian子流形上的clasp移动如何作用于伪全纯曲线模空间，进而影响Chekanov-Eliashberg代数微分；并利用该工具构造在augmentation意义下存在但在Lagrangian填充意义下不存在的Legendrian示例，以揭示两者的深层差异。

Method: 通过分析clasp移动对贡献于Chekanov-Eliashberg代数微分的伪全纯曲线模空间的几何影响，系统地计算twist spheres Λ_k的微分级数；并基于该计算对任意水平可置换的Legendrian Λ 构造一个新的Legendrian族 T(Λ,k)。

Result: 成功计算了高维twist spheres的Chekanov-Eliashberg代数微分；构造了无限族Legendrian T(Λ,k)，证明其CE代数具有augmentation，但证明了当k充分大时不存在具有消失Maslov类的精确嵌入Lagrangian填充。

Conclusion: 本文建立了clasp移动与高维Legendrian不变量之间的联系，并构造了新的例子表明：存在augmentation与存在精确Lagrangian填充这两个条件在高维中可以分离——即augmentation的存在性并不能保证精确Lagrangian填充的存在性。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of the clasp move of Legendrian submanifolds of dimension at least two on the moduli spaces of pseudo-holomorphic curves that contribute to the differential of the Chekanov-Eliashberg algebra. As an application, we compute the differential of Chekanov-Eliashberg algebra of twist spheres $Λ_k$ of dimension at least two. Moreover, we construct an infinite family of Legendrians $\mathcal{T}(Λ,k)$ from any horizontally displaceable Legendrian $Λ$ in $P\times\mathbb{R}$, such that the Chekanov-Eliashberg algebra of $\mathcal{T}(Λ,k)$ admits an augmentation, while $\mathcal{T}(Λ,k)$ has no exact embedded Lagrangian filling of vanishing Maslov class in the symplectization of $P\times \mathbb{R}$ for sufficiently large $k$.

</details>
